    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Threading;
    4: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Pooling;

   12: namespace WildsEngine.Core
   13: {
   14:     /// <summary>
   15:     /// Composition root for one isolated Wilds runtime instance.
   16:     ///
   17:     /// Wilds accepts complete requests as Pulses, owns their
   18:     /// internal WorkUnits, resolves them into Moments, and
   19:     /// records those Moments in its Timeline and Epoch.
   20:     /// </summary>
   21:     public sealed class WildsEngine :
   22:         IDisposable
   23:     {
   24:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   25: 
   26:         private readonly DefaultInstructionsRegistry
   27:             _instructionRegistry;
   28: 
   29:         private readonly DefaultTasksRegistry
   30:             _tasksRegistry;
   31: 
   32:         private readonly ObjectPool<ITaskProcessor>
   33:             _taskProcessors;
   34: 
   35:         private readonly ObjectPool<IInstructionsProcessor>
   36:             _instructionsProcessors;
   37: 
   38:         private readonly Timeline _timeline;
   39:         private readonly Epoch _epoch;
   40: 
   41:         private int _disposed;
   42: 
   43:         public WildsEngine(
   44:             IComponent component,
   45:             WildsEngineOptions options)
   46:         {
   47:             _component =
   48:                 component ??
   49:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   50:                     nameof(component));
   51: 
   52:             if (options == null)
   53:             {
   54:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   55:                     nameof(options));
   56:             }
   57: 
   58:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   59:                     options.Layer))
   60:             {
   61:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   62:                     "The engine requires a layer.",
   63:                     nameof(options));
   64:             }
   65: 
   66:             Layer =
   67:                 options.Layer.Trim();
   68: 
   69:             _instructionRegistry =
   70:                 new DefaultInstructionsRegistry(
   71:                     _component,
   72:                     $"{Layer}.instruction-registry");
   73: 
   74:             _tasksRegistry =
   75:                 new DefaultTasksRegistry(
   76:                     _component,
   77:                     $"{Layer}.task-registry");
   78: 
   79:             _taskProcessors =
   80:                 new ObjectPool<ITaskProcessor>(
   81:                     factory: () =>
   82:                         new TaskProcessor(
   83:                             _component,
   84:                             $"{Layer}.task-processor",
   85:                             _tasksRegistry),
   86:                     maximumRetained:
   87:                         options.RetainedTaskProcessors,
   88:                     maximumConcurrent:
   89:                         options.ConcurrentTaskProcessors);
   90: 
   91:             _instructionsProcessors =
   92:                 new ObjectPool<IInstructionsProcessor>(
   93:                     factory: () =>
   94:                         new InstructionsProcessor(
   95:                             _component,
   96:                             $"{Layer}.instructions-processor",
   97:                             _instructionRegistry,
   98:                             _taskProcessors),
   99:                     maximumRetained:
  100:                         options.RetainedInstructionsProcessors,
  101:                     maximumConcurrent:
  102:                         options.ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors);
  103: 
  104:             _timeline =
  105:                 new Timeline();
  106: 
  107:             _epoch =
  108:                 new Epoch();
  109: 
  110:             if (!_epoch.AddTimeline(
  111:                     _timeline))
  112:             {
  113:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  114:                     "The engine could not initialize " +
  115:                     "its primary Timeline.");
  116:             }
  117:         }
  118: 
  119:         public string Layer { get; }
  120: 
  121:         public IComponent Component =>
  122:             _component;
  123: 
  124:         public bool IsInitialized { get; private set; }
  125:

  127:             Instructions =>

  144:         public bool Initialize()
  145:         {
  146:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  147: 
  148:             if (IsInitialized)
  149:                 return false;
  150: 
  151:             _instructionRegistry.Initialize();
  152:             _tasksRegistry.Initialize();
  153: 
  154:             IsInitialized =
  155:                 true;
  156: 
  157:             return true;

  159: 
  160:         /// <summary>
  161:         /// Executes one complete consumer request through the
  162:         /// certified single-WorkUnit Pulse lifecycle.
  163:         ///
  164:         /// Wilds creates and correlates the Pulse and WorkUnit,
  165:         /// owns their lifecycle transitions, finalizes the
  166:         /// terminal Moment, and records that Moment in the
  167:         /// engine Timeline.
  168:         ///
  169:         /// The supplied operation represents internal work
  170:         /// performed by Wilds or an integration adapter.
  171:         /// </summary>
  172:         public async ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
  173:             object? request,
  174:             Func<
  175:                 object?,
  176:                 CancellationToken,
  177:                 ValueTask<object?>> operation,
  178:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
  179:         {
  180:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  181:             ThrowIfNotInitialized();
  182: 
  183:             if (operation == null)
  184:             {
  185:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  186:                     nameof(operation));
  187:             }
  188: 
  189:             var pulse =
  190:                 new Pulse(
  191:                     request);
  192: 
  193:             var work =
  194:                 new WorkUnit(
  195:                     pulse.Id,
  196:                     request);
  197: 
  198:             var coordinator =
  199:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  200:                     pulse);
  201: 
  202:             BeginPulse(
  203:                 pulse,
  204:                 work,
  205:                 coordinator);
  206: 
  207:             object? result;
  208: 
  209:             try
  210:             {
  211:                 cancellationToken
  212:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  213: 
  214:                 result =
  215:                     await operation(
  216:                             request,
  217:                             cancellationToken)
  218:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  219: 
  220:                 cancellationToken
  221:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  222: 
  223:                 if (!work.TryComplete(
  224:                         result))
  225:                 {
  226:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  227:                         "The WorkUnit could not complete.");
  228:                 }
  229:             }
  230:             catch (OperationCanceledException)
  231:             {
  232:                 if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  233:                     !work.TryCancel())
  234:                 {
  235:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  236:                         "The WorkUnit could not be cancelled.");
  237:                 }
  238: 
  239:                 var cancelledMoment =
  240:                     FinalizePulse(
  241:                         coordinator,
  242:                         null);
  243: 
  244:                 RecordMoment(
  245:                     cancelledMoment);
  246: 
  247:                 return cancelledMoment;
  248:             }
  249:             catch (Exception exception)
  250:             {
  251:                 if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  252:                     !work.TryFail(
  253:                         exception))
  254:                 {
  255:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  256:                         "The WorkUnit could not record failure.",
  257:                         exception);
  258:                 }
  259: 
  260:                 var failedMoment =
  261:                     FinalizePulse(
  262:                         coordinator,
  263:                         null);
  264: 
  265:                 RecordMoment(
  266:                     failedMoment);
  267: 
  268:                 return failedMoment;
  269:             }
  270: 
  271:             var completedMoment =
  272:                 FinalizePulse(
  273:                     coordinator,
  274:                     result);
  275: 
  276:             RecordMoment(
  277:                 completedMoment);
  278: 
  279:             return completedMoment;
  280:         }
  281: 
  282:         /// <summary>
  283:         /// Executes one complete consumer request as multiple
  284:         /// required internal pieces of work.
  285:         ///
  286:         /// Wilds creates and owns the Pulse, every required
  287:         /// WorkUnit, coordination, terminal outcome, Moment,
  288:         /// and temporal history.
  289:         ///
  290:         /// The result selector explicitly chooses the one
  291:         /// authoritative Pulse result after every required
  292:         /// operation has produced its subordinate result.
  293:         /// </summary>
  294:         public async ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
  295:             object? request,
  296:             IReadOnlyList<
  297:                 Func<
  298:                     object?,
  299:                     CancellationToken,
  300:                     ValueTask<object?>>> operations,
  301:             Func<
  302:                 IReadOnlyList<object?>,
  303:                 object?> resultSelector,
  304:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
  305:         {
  306:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  307:             ThrowIfNotInitialized();
  308: 
  309:             if (operations == null)
  310:             {
  311:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  312:                     nameof(operations));
  313:             }
  314: 
  315:             if (operations.Count == 0)
  316:             {
  317:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  318:                     "At least one operation is required.",
  319:                     nameof(operations));
  320:             }
  321: 
  322:             if (resultSelector == null)
  323:             {
  324:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  325:                     nameof(resultSelector));
  326:             }
  327: 
  328:             for (var index = 0;
  329:                  index < operations.Count;
  330:                  index++)
  331:             {
  332:                 if (operations[index] == null)
  333:                 {
  334:                     throw new ArgumentException(
  335:                         "Every operation must be non-null.",
  336:                         nameof(operations));
  337:                 }
  338:             }
  339: 
  340:             var pulse =
  341:                 new Pulse(
  342:                     request);
  343: 
  344:             var coordinator =
  345:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  346:                     pulse);
  347: 
  348:             var workUnits =
  349:                 new WorkUnit[operations.Count];
  350: 
  351:             var results =
  352:                 new object?[operations.Count];
  353: 
  354:             BeginDecomposedPulse(
  355:                 pulse,
  356:                 workUnits,
  357:                 coordinator,
  358:                 request);
  359: 
  360:             for (var index = 0;
  361:                  index < operations.Count;
  362:                  index++)
  363:             {
  364:                 var work =
  365:                     workUnits[index];
  366: 
  367:                 try
  368:                 {
  369:                     cancellationToken
  370:                         .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  371: 
  372:                     if (!work.TryBeginProcessing())
  373:                     {
  374:                         throw new InvalidOperationException(
  375:                             "The WorkUnit could not begin processing.");
  376:                     }
  377: 
  378:                     var result =
  379:                         await operations[index](
  380:                                 request,
  381:                                 cancellationToken)
  382:                             .ConfigureAwait(false);
  383: 
  384:                     cancellationToken
  385:                         .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  386: 
  387:                     results[index] =
  388:                         result;
  389: 
  390:                     /*
  391:                      * The final WorkUnit remains Processing
  392:                      * until the authoritative Pulselected.
  393:                      *
  394:                      * Earlier WorkUnits may complete immediately
  395:                      * because their results are subordinate.
  396:                      */
  397:                     if (index <
  398:                         operations.Count - 1)
  399:                     {
  400:                         if (!work.TryComplete(
  401:                                 result))
  402:                         {
  403:                             throw new InvalidOperationException(
  404:                                 "The WorkUnit could not complete.");
  405:                         }
  406:                     }
  407:                 }
  408:                 catch (OperationCanceledException)
  409:                 {
  410:                     if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  411:                         !work.TryCancel())
  412:                     {
  413:                         throw new InvalidOperationException(
  414:                             "The active WorkUnit could not be cancelled.");
  415:                     }
  416: 
  417:                     CancelRemainingWork(
  418:                         workUnits,
  419:                         index + 1);
  420: 
  421:                     var cancelledMoment =
  422:                         FinalizePulse(
  423:                             coordinator,
  424:                             null);
  425: 
  426:                     RecordMoment(
  427:                         cancelledMoment);
  428: 
  429:                     return cancelledMoment;
  430:                 }
  431:                 catch (Exception exception)
  432:                 {
  433:                     if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  434:                         !work.TryFail(
  435:                             exception))
  436:                     {
  437:                         throw new InvalidOperationException(
  438:                             "The active WorkUnit could not record failure.",
  439:                             exception);
  440:                     }
  441: 
  442:                     CancelRemainingWork(
  443:                         workUnits,
  444:                         index + 1);
  445: 
  446:                     var failedMoment =
  447:                         FinalizePulse(
  448:                             coordinator,
  449:                             null);
  450: 
  451:                     RecordMoment(
  452:                         failedMoment);
  453: 
  454:                     return failedMoment;
  455:                 }
  456:             }
  457: 
  458:             var finalWork =
  459:                 workUnits[
  460:                     workUnits.Length - 1];
  461: 
  462:             object? finalResult;
  463: 
  464:             try
  465:             {
  466:                 cancellationToken
  467:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  468: 
  469:                 finalResult =
  470:                     resultSelector(
  471:                         results);
  472: 
  473:                 cancellationToken
  474:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  475: 
  476:                 if (!finalWork.TryComplete(
  477:                         results[
  478:                             results.Length - 1]))
  479:                 {
  480:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  481:                         "The final WorkUnit could not complete.");
  482:                 }
  483:             }
  484:             catch (OperationCanceledException)
  485:             {
  486:                 if (!finalWork.IsTerminal &&
  487:                     !finalWork.TryCancel())
  488:                 {
  489:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  490:                         "The final WorkUnit could not be cancelled.");
  491:                 }
  492: 
  493:                 var cancelledMoment =
  494:                     FinalizePulse(
  495:                         coordinator,
  496:                         null);
  497: 
  498:                 RecordMoment(
  499:                     cancelledMoment);
  500: 
  501:                 return cancelledMoment;
  502:             }
  503:             catch (Exception exception)
  504:             {
  505:                 if (!finalWork.IsTerminal &&
  506:                     !finalWork.TryFail(
  507:                         exception))
  508:                 {
  509:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  510:                         "The final WorkUnit could not record failure.",
  511:                         exception);
  512:                 }
  513: 
  514:                 var failedMoment =
  515:                     FinalizePulse(
  516:                         coordinator,
  517:                         null);
  518: 
  519:                 RecordMoment(
  520:                     failedMoment);
  521: 
  522:                 return failedMoment;
  523:             }
  524: 
  525:             var completedMoment =
  526:                 FinalizePulse(
  527:                     coordinator,
  528:                     finalResult);
  529: 
  530:             RecordMoment(
  531:                 completedMoment);
  532: 
  533:             return completedMoment;
  534:         }
  535: 
  536:         /// <summary>
  537:         /// Preserves the existing certified instruction
  538:         /// processor lease boundary.
  539:         /// </summary>
  540:         public async ValueTask<TResult>
  541:             ExecuteInstructionAsync<TResult>(
  542:                 Func<
  543:                     IInstructionsProcessor,
  544:                     CancellationToken,
  545:                     ValueTask<TResult>> operation,
  546:                 CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
  547:         {
  548:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  549:             ThrowIfNotInitialized();
  550: 
  551:             if (operation == null)
  552:             {
  553:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  554:                     nameof(operation));
  555:             }
  556: 
  557:             using (var lease =
  558:                 await _instructionsProcessors
  559:                     .RentAsync(
  560:                         cancellationToken)
  561:                     .ConfigureAwait(false))
  562:             {
  563:                 return await operation(
  564:                         lease.Instance,
  565:                         cancellationToken)
  566:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  567:             }
  568:         }
  569: 
  570:         public bool Shutdown()
  571:         {
  572:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  573: 
  574:             if (!IsInitialized)
  575:                 return false;
  576: 
  577:             _tasksRegistry.Shutdown();
  578:             _instructionRegistry.Shutdown();
  579: 
  580:             IsInitialized =
  581:                 false;
  582: 
  583:             return true;
  584:         }
  585: 
  586:         public void Dispose()
  587:         {
  588:             if (Interlocked.Exchange(
  589:                     ref _disposed,
  590:                     1) != 0)
  591:             {
  592:                 return;
  593:             }
  594: 
  595:             if (IsInitialized)
  596:             {
  597:                 _tasksRegistry.Shutdown();
  598:                 _instructionRegistry.Shutdown();
  599: 
  600:                 IsInitialized =
  601:                     false;
  602:             }
  603: 
  604:             _instructionsProcessors.Dispose();
  605:             _taskProcessors.Dispose();
  606:         }
  607: 
  608:         private static void BeginPulse(
  609:             Pulse pulse,
  610:             WorkUnit work,
  611:             IPulseCoordinator coordinator)
  612:         {
  613:             if (!pulse.TryBeginDecomposition())
  614:             {
  615:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  616:                     "The Pulse could not begin decomposition.");
  617:             }
  618: 
  619:             if (!coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  620:                     work))
  621:             {
  622:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  623:                     "The WorkUnit could not be correlated " +
  624:                     "with its Pulse.");
  625:             }
  626: 
  627:             if (!work.TryMarkPending())
  628:             {
  629:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  630:                     "The WorkUnit could not become pending.");
  631:             }
  632: 
  633:             if (!pulse.TryMarkPending())
  634:             {
  635:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  636:                     "The Pulse could not become pending.");
  637:             }
  638: 
  639:             if (!pulse.TryMarkReady())
  640:             {
  641:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  642:                     "The Pulse could not become ready.");
  643:             }
  644: 
  645:             if (!pulse.TryBeginProcessing())
  646:             {
  647:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  648:                     "The Pulse could not begin processing.");
  649:             }
  650: 
  651:             if (!work.TryBeginProcessing())
  652:             {
  653:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  654:                     "The WorkUnit could not begin processing.");
  655:             }
  656:         }
  657: 
  658:         private static void BeginDecomposedPulse(
  659:             Pulse pulse,
  660:             WorkUnit[] workUnits,
  661:             IPulseCoordinator coordinator,
  662:             object? request)
  663:         {
  664:             if (!pulse.TryBeginDecomposition())
  665:             {
  666:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  667:                     "The Pulse could not begin decomposition.");
  668:             }
  669: 
  670:             for (var index = 0;
  671:                  index < workUnits.Length;
  672:                  index++)
  673:             {
  674:                 var work =
  675:                     new WorkUnit(
  676:                         pulse.Id,
  677:                         request);
  678: 
  679:                 workUnits[index] =
  680:                     work;
  681: 
  682:                 if (!coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  683:                         work))
  684:                 {
  685:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  686:                         "A WorkUnit could not be correlated " +
  687:                         "with its Pulse.");
  688:                 }
  689: 
  690:                 if (!work.TryMarkPending())
  691:                 {
  692:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  693:                         "A WorkUnit could not become pending.");
  694:                 }
  695:             }
  696: 
  697:             if (!pulse.TryMarkPending())
  698:             {
  699:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  700:                     "The Pulse could not become pending.");
  701:             }
  702: 
  703:             if (!pulse.TryMarkReady())
  704:             {
  705:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  706:                     "The Pulse could not become ready.");
  707:             }
  708: 
  709:             if (!pulse.TryBeginProcessing())
  710:             {
  711:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  712:                     "The Pulse could not begin processing.");
  713:             }
  714:         }
  715: 
  716:         private static void CancelRemainingWork(
  717:             WorkUnit[] workUnits,
  718:             int startIndex)
  719:         {
  720:             for (var index = startIndex;
  721:                  index < workUnits.Length;
  722:                  index++)
  723:             {
  724:                 var work =
  725:                     workUnits[index];
  726: 
  727:                 if (work.IsTerminal)
  728:                 {
  729:                     continue;
  730:                 }
  731: 
  732:                 if (!work.TryCancel())
  733:                 {
  734:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  735:                         "A remaining WorkUnit could not be cancelled.");
  736:                 }
  737:             }
  738:         }
  739: 
  740:         private static IMoment FinalizePulse(
  741:             IPulseCoordinator coordinator,
  742:             object? result)
  743:         {
  744:             if (!coordinator.CanFinalize)
  745:             {
  746:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  747:                     "The Pulse cannot finalize while required " +
  748:                     "WorkUnits remain unresolved.");
  749:             }
  750: 
  751:             if (!coordinator.TryFinalize(
  752:                     result,
  753:                     out var moment))
  754:             {
  755:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  756:                     "The Pulse coordinator could not create " +
  757:                     "a terminal Moment.");
  758:             }
  759: 
  760:             return moment ??
  761:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  762:                     "Pulse finalization returned no Moment.");
  763:         }
  764: 
  765:         private void RecordMoment(
  766:             IMoment moment)
  767:         {
  768:             if (!_timeline.AddMoment(
  769:                     moment))
  770:             {
  771:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  772:                     "The terminal Moment could not be " +
  773:                     "recorded in the engine Timeline.");
  774:             }
  775:         }
  776: 
  777:         private void ThrowIfNotInitialized()
  778:         {
  779:             if (!IsInitialized)
  780:             {
  781:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  782:                     "The WildsEngine must be initialized " +
  783:                     "before processing requests.");
  784:             }
  785:         }
  786: 
  787:         private void ThrowIfDisposed()
  788:         {
  789:             if (Volatile.Read(
  790:                     ref _disposed) != 0)
  791:             {
  792:                 throw new ObjectDisposedException(
  793:                     nameof(WildsEngine));
  794:             }
  795:         }
  796:     }
  797: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\BaseUnit.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Provides stable identity for Wilds temporal and
    8:     /// processing units.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Units intentionally do not inherit from BaseObject.
   11:     /// A unit is not required to own a component, state,
   12:     /// signal, metadata collection, or neighbor graph.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public abstract class BaseUnit :
   15:         IBaseUnit
   16:     {
   17:         protected BaseUnit()
   18:             : this(
   19:                 Guid.NewGuid())
   20:         {
   21:         }
   22: 
   23:         protected BaseUnit(
   24:             Guid id)
   25:         {
   26:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   27:             {
   28:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   29:                     "Wilds units require a non-empty GUID.",
   30:                     nameof(id));
   31:             }
   32: 
   33:             Id = id;
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         public Guid Id { get; }
   37:     }
   38: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\Pulse.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents one complete consumer request.
    8:     ///
    9:     /// The pulse identity is the canonical correlation
   10:     /// identity for every work unit, instruction, task,
   11:     /// and eventual moment produced while resolving the
   12:     /// request.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class Pulse :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IPulse
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private PulseStatus _status;
   22: 
   23:         public Pulse(
   24:             object? request)
   25:             : base()
   26:         {
   27:             Request = request;
   28: 
   29:             _status =
   30:                 PulseStatus.Created;
   31:         }
   32: 
   33:         public Pulse(
   34:             Guid id,
   35:             object? request)
   36:             : base(
   37:                 id)
   38:         {
   39:             Request = request;
   40: 
   41:             _status =
   42:                 PulseStatus.Created;
   43:         }
   44: 
   45:         public object? Request { get; }
   46: 
   47:         public PulseStatus Status
   48:         {
   49:             get
   50:             {
   51:                 lock (_sync)
   52:                 {
   53:                     return _status;
   54:                 }
   55:             }
   56:         }
   57: 
   58:         public bool IsTerminal
   59:         {
   60:             get
   61:             {
   62:                 lock (_sync)
   63:                 {
   64:                     return IsTerminalStatus(
   65:                         _status);
   66:                 }
   67:             }
   68:         }
   69: 
   70:         public bool TryBeginDecomposition()
   71:         {
   72:             return TryTransition(
   73:                 PulseStatus.Created,
   74:                 PulseStatus.Decomposing);
   75:         }
   76: 
   77:         public bool TryMarkPending()
   78:         {
   79:             return TryTransition(
   80:                 PulseStatus.Decomposing,
   81:                 PulseStatus.Pending);
   82:         }
   83: 
   84:         public bool TryMarkReady()
   85:         {
   86:             return TryTransition(
   87:                 PulseStatus.Pending,
   88:                 PulseStatus.Ready);
   89:         }
   90: 
   91:         public bool TryBeginProcessing()
   92:         {
   93:             return TryTransition(
   94:                 PulseStatus.Ready,
   95:                 PulseStatus.Processing);
   96:         }
   97: 
   98:         public bool TryComplete()
   99:         {
  100:             return TryTransition(
  101:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  102:                 PulseStatus.Completed);
  103:         }
  104: 
  105:         public bool TryFail()
  106:         {
  107:             return TryTerminalTransition(
  108:                 PulseStatus.Failed);
  109:         }
  110: 
  111:         public bool TryCancel()
  112:         {
  113:             return TryTerminalTransition(
  114:                 PulseStatus.Cancelled);
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         private bool TryTransition(
  118:             PulseStatus expected,
  119:             PulseStatus next)
  120:         {
  121:             lock (_sync)
  122:             {
  123:                 if (_status != expected)
  124:                 {
  125:                     return false;
  126:                 }
  127: 
  128:                 _status = next;
  129: 
  130:                 return true;
  131:             }
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         private bool TryTerminalTransition(
  135:             PulseStatus terminalStatus)
  136:         {
  137:             lock (_sync)
  138:             {
  139:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  140:                         _status))
  141:                 {
  142:                     return false;
  143:                 }
  144: 
  145:                 _status = terminalStatus;
  146: 
  147:                 return true;
  148:             }
  149:         }
  150: 
  151:         private static bool IsTerminalStatus(
  152:             PulseStatus status)
  153:         {
  154:             return status ==
  155:                    PulseStatus.Completed ||
  156:                    status ==
  157:                    PulseStatus.Failed ||
  158:                    status ==
  159:                    PulseStatus.Cancelled;
  160:         }
  161:     }
  162: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\WorkUnit.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents one internal piece of processing required
    8:     /// to resolve a pulse.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Work units are implementation details of Wilds.
   11:     /// Consumers submit pulses and are not responsible for
   12:     /// estimating, creating, or scheduling work units.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class WorkUnit :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IWorkUnit
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private WorkUnitStatus _status;
   22:         private object? _result;
   23:         private Exception? _exception;
   24: 
   25:         public WorkUnit(
   26:             Guid pulseId,
   27:             object? payload)
   28:             : base()
   29:         {
   30:             PulseId =
   31:                 ValidatePulseId(
   32:                     pulseId);
   33: 
   34:             Payload = payload;
   35: 
   36:             _status =
   37:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created;
   38:         }
   39: 
   40:         public WorkUnit(
   41:             Guid id,
   42:             Guid pulseId,
   43:             object? payload)
   44:             : base(
   45:                 id)
   46:         {
   47:             PulseId =
   48:                 ValidatePulseId(
   49:                     pulseId);
   50: 
   51:             Payload = payload;
   52: 
   53:             _status =
   54:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created;
   55:         }
   56: 
   57:         public Guid PulseId { get; }
   58: 
   59:         public object? Payload { get; }
   60: 
   61:         public WorkUnitStatus Status
   62:         {
   63:             get
   64:             {
   65:                 lock (_sync)
   66:                 {
   67:                     return _status;
   68:                 }
   69:             }
   70:         }
   71: 
   72:         public object? Result
   73:         {
   74:             get
   75:             {
   76:                 lock (_sync)
   77:                 {
   78:                     return _result;
   79:                 }
   80:             }
   81:         }
   82: 
   83:         public Exception? Exception
   84:         {
   85:             get
   86:             {
   87:                 lock (_sync)
   88:                 {
   89:                     return _exception;
   90:                 }
   91:             }
   92:         }
   93: 
   94:         public bool IsTerminal
   95:         {
   96:             get
   97:             {
   98:                 lock (_sync)
   99:                 {
  100:                     return IsTerminalStatus(
  101:                         _status);
  102:                 }
  103:             }
  104:         }
  105: 
  106:         public bool TryMarkPending()
  107:         {
  108:             return TryTransition(
  109:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created,
  110:                 WorkUnitStatus.Pending);
  111:         }
  112: 
  113:         public bool TryBeginProcessing()
  114:         {
  115:             return TryTransition(
  116:                 WorkUnitStatus.Pending,
  117:                 WorkUnitStatus.Processing);
  118:         }
  119: 
  120:         public bool TryComplete(
  121:             object? result)
  122:         {
  123:             lock (_sync)
  124:             {
  125:                 if (_status !=
  126:                     WorkUnitStatus.Processing)
  127:                 {
  128:                     return false;
  129:                 }
  130: 
  131:                 _result = result;
  132:                 _exception = null;
  133: 
  134:                 _status =
  135:                     WorkUnitStatus.Completed;
  136: 
  137:                 return true;
  138:             }
  139:         }
  140: 
  141:         public bool TryFail(
  142:             Exception exception)
  143:         {
  144:             if (exception == null)
  145:             {
  146:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  147:                     nameof(exception));
  148:             }
  149: 
  150:             lock (_sync)
  151:             {
  152:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  153:                         _status))
  154:                 {
  155:                     return false;
  156:                 }
  157: 
  158:                 _exception = exception;
  159: 
  160:                 _status =
  161:                     WorkUnitStatus.Failed;
  162: 
  163:                 return true;
  164:             }
  165:         }
  166: 
  167:         public bool TryCancel()
  168:         {
  169:             lock (_sync)
  170:             {
  171:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  172:                         _status))
  173:                 {
  174:                     return false;
  175:                 }
  176: 
  177:                 _status =
  178:                     WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled;
  179: 
  180:                 return true;
  181:             }
  182:         }
  183: 
  184:         private bool TryTransition(
  185:             WorkUnitStatus expected,
  186:             WorkUnitStatus next)
  187:         {
  188:             lock (_sync)
  189:             {
  190:                 if (_status != expected)
  191:                 {
  192:                     return false;
  193:                 }
  194: 
  195:                 _status = next;
  196: 
  197:                 return true;
  198:             }
  199:         }
  200: 
  201:         private static Guid ValidatePulseId(
  202:             Guid pulseId)
  203:         {
  204:             if (pulseId == Guid.Empty)
  205:             {
  206:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  207:                     "Work units require a non-empty pulse ID.",
  208:                     nameof(pulseId));
  209:             }
  210: 
  211:             return pulseId;
  212:         }
  213: 
  214:         private static bool IsTerminalStatus(
  215:             WorkUnitStatus status)
  216:         {
  217:             return status ==
  218:                    WorkUnitStatus.Completed ||
  219:                    status ==
  220:                    WorkUnitStatus.Failed ||
  221:                    status ==
  222:                    WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled;
  223:         }
  224:     }
  225: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\Moment.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Linq;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    7: {
    8:     /// <summary>
    9:     /// Immutable record of a terminal Pulse outcome.
   10:     ///
   11:     /// A Moment connects the Pulse identity, contributing
   12:     /// WorkUnits, final result, and terminal outcome.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class Moment :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IMoment
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly IReadOnlyList<Guid>
   19:             _workUnitIds;
   20: 
   21:         public Moment(
   22:             Guid pulseId,
   23:             object? result,
   24:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   25:             : this(
   26:                 Guid.NewGuid(),
   27:                 pulseId,
   28:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   29:                 result,
   30:                 null,
   31:                 workUnitIds)
   32:         {
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         public Moment(
   36:             Guid id,
   37:             Guid pulseId,
   38:             object? result,
   39:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   40:             : this(
   41:                 id,
   42:                 pulseId,
   43:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   44:                 result,
   45:                 null,
   46:                 workUnitIds)
   47:         {
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         public Moment(
   51:             Guid pulseId,
   52:             MomentOutcome outcome,
   53:             object? result,
   54:             Exception? exception,
   55:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   56:             : this(
   57:                 Guid.NewGuid(),
   58:                 pulseId,
   59:                 outcome,
   60:                 result,
   61:                 exception,
   62:                 workUnitIds)
   63:         {
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         public Moment(
   67:             Guid id,
   68:             Guid pulseId,
   69:             MomentOutcome outcome,
   70:             object? result,
   71:             Exception? exception,
   72:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   73:             : base(
   74:                 id)
   75:         {
   76:             PulseId =
   77:                 ValidatePulseId(
   78:                     pulseId);
   79: 
   80:             ValidateOutcome(
   81:                 outcome,
   82:                 exception);
   83: 
   84:             Outcome = outcome;
   85:             Result = result;
   86:             Exception = exception;
   87: 
   88:             _workUnitIds =
   89:                 CopyWorkUnitIds(
   90:                     workUnitIds);
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         public Guid PulseId { get; }
   94: 
   95:         public MomentOutcome Outcome { get; }
   96: 
   97:         public object? Result { get; }
   98: 
   99:         public Exception? Exception { get; }
  100: 
  101:         public IReadOnlyList<Guid> WorkUnitIds =>
  102:             _workUnitIds;
  103: 
  104:         private static Guid ValidatePulseId(
  105:             Guid pulseId)
  106:         {
  107:             if (pulseId == Guid.Empty)
  108:             {
  109:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  110:                     "Moments require a non-empty Pulse ID.",
  111:                     nameof(pulseId));
  112:             }
  113: 
  114:             return pulseId;
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         private static void ValidateOutcome(
  118:             MomentOutcome outcome,
  119:             Exception? exception)
  120:         {
  121:             if (outcome ==
  122:                 MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  123:                 exception == null)
  124:             {
  125:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  126:                     "Failed Moments require an exception.",
  127:                     nameof(exception));
  128:             }
  129: 
  130:             if (outcome !=
  131:                 MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  132:                 exception != null)
  133:             {
  134:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  135:                     "Only failed Moments may carry an exception.",
  136:                     nameof(exception));
  137:             }
  138:         }
  139: 
  140:         private static IReadOnlyList<Guid>
  141:             CopyWorkUnitIds(
  142:                 IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds)
  143:         {
  144:             if (workUnitIds == null)
  145:             {
  146:                 return Array.Empty<Guid>();
  147:             }
  148: 
  149:             var copy =
  150:                 workUnitIds
  151:                     .Where(
  152:                         id => id != Guid.Empty)
  153:                     .Distinct()
  154:                     .ToArray();
  155: 
  156:             return copy.Length == 0
  157:                 ? Array.Empty<Guid>()
  158:                 : copy;
  159:         }
  160:     }
  161: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\Timeline.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Maintains an ordered collection of completed moments.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Timeline order reflects the order in which moments
   11:     /// are recorded. It does not depend on system clock time.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public sealed class Timeline :
   14:         BaseUnit,
   15:         ITimeline
   16:     {
   17:         private readonly object _sync =
   18:             new object();
   19: 
   20:         private readonly List<IMoment> _moments =
   21:             new List<IMoment>();
   22: 
   23:         private readonly HashSet<Guid> _momentIds =
   24:             new HashSet<Guid>();
   25: 
   26:         public Timeline()
   27:             : base()
   28:         {
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public Timeline(
   32:             Guid id)
   33:             : base(
   34:                 id)
   35:         {
   36:         }
   37: 
   38:         public int Count
   39:         {
   40:             get
   41:             {
   42:                 lock (_sync)
   43:                 {
   44:                     return _moments.Count;
   45:                 }
   46:             }
   47:         }
   48: 
   49:         public IReadOnlyList<IMoment> Moments
   50:         {
   51:             get
   52:             {
   53:                 lock (_sync)
   54:                 {
   55:                     return _moments.ToArray();
   56:                 }
   57:             }
   58:         }
   59: 
   60:         public bool AddMoment(
   61:             IMoment moment)
   62:         {
   63:             if (moment == null)
   64:             {
   65:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   66:                     nameof(moment));
   67:             }
   68: 
   69:             lock (_sync)
   70:             {
   71:                 if (!_momentIds.Add(
   72:                         moment.Id))
   73:                 {
   74:                     return false;
   75:                 }
   76: 
   77:                 _moments.Add(
   78:                     moment);
   79: 
   80:                 return true;
   81:             }
   82:         }
   83:     }
   84: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Coordinator\PulseCoordinator.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Linq;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Coordinator
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Coordinates all required WorkUnits for one Pulse.
   11:     ///
   12:     /// The coordinator prevents early completion and records
   13:     /// successful, failed, and cancelled Pulse outcomes.
   14:     /// </summary>
   15:     public sealed class PulseCoordinator :
   16:         IPulseCoordinator
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private readonly List<IWorkUnit> _workUnits =
   22:             new List<IWorkUnit>();
   23: 
   24:         private readonly HashSet<Guid> _workUnitIds =
   25:             new HashSet<Guid>();
   26: 
   27:         public PulseCoordinator(
   28:             IPulse pulse)
   29:         {
   30:             Pulse = pulse ??
   31:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   32:                     nameof(pulse));
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         public IPulse Pulse { get; }
   36: 
   37:         public int WorkUnitCount
   38:         {
   39:             get
   40:             {
   41:                 lock (_sync)
   42:                 {
   43:                     return _workUnits.Count;
   44:                 }
   45:             }
   46:         }
   47: 
   48:         public IReadOnlyList<IWorkUnit> WorkUnits
   49:         {
   50:             get
   51:             {
   52:                 lock (_sync)
   53:                 {
   54:                     return _workUnits.ToArray();
   55:                 }
   56:             }
   57:         }
   58: 
   59:         public bool CanResolve
   60:         {
   61:             get
   62:             {
   63:                 lock (_sync)
   64:                 {
   65:                     return CanResolveCore();
   66:                 }
   67:             }
   68:         }
   69: 
   70:         public bool CanFinalize
   71:         {
   72:             get
   73:             {
   74:                 lock (_sync)
   75:                 {
   76:                     return CanFinalizeCore();
   77:                 }
   78:             }
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         public bool AddWorkUnit(
   82:             IWorkUnit workUnit)
   83:         {
   84:             if (workUnit == null)
   85:             {
   86:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   87:                     nameof(workUnit));
   88:             }
   89: 
   90:             if (workUnit.PulseId != Pulse.Id)
   91:             {
   92:                 return false;
   93:             }
   94: 
   95:             lock (_sync)
   96:             {
   97:                 if (Pulse.IsTerminal)
   98:                 {
   99:                     return false;
  100:                 }
  101: 
  102:                 if (!_workUnitIds.Add(
  103:                         workUnit.Id))
  104:                 {
  105:                     return false;
  106:                 }
  107: 
  108:                 _workUnits.Add(
  109:                     workUnit);
  110: 
  111:                 return true;
  112:             }
  113:         }
  114: 
  115:         public bool TryResolve(
  116:             object? result,
  117:             out IMoment? moment)
  118:         {
  119:             lock (_sync)
  120:             {
  121:                 moment = null;
  122: 
  123:                 if (!CanResolveCore())
  124:                 {
  125:                     return false;
  126:                 }
  127: 
  128:                 return TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  129:                     result,
  130:                     out moment);
  131:             }
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         public bool TryFinalize(
  135:             object? result,
  136:             out IMoment? moment)
  137:         {
  138:             lock (_sync)
  139:             {
  140:                 moment = null;
  141: 
  142:                 if (!CanFinalizeCore())
  143:                 {
  144:                     return false;
  145:                 }
  146: 
  147:                 var failedWork =
  148:                     GetFirstFailedWorkUnit();
  149: 
  150:                 if (failedWork != null)
  151:                 {
  152:                     return TryCreateFailedMoment(
  153:                         failedWork,
  154:                         out moment);
  155:                 }
  156: 
  157:                 if (ContainsCancelledWorkUnit())
  158:                 {
  159:                     return TryCreateCancelledMoment(
  160:                         out moment);
  161:                 }
  162: 
  163:                 return TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  164:                     result,
  165:                     out moment);
  166:             }
  167:         }
  168: 
  169:         private bool TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  170:             object? result,
  171:             out IMoment? moment)
  172:         {
  173:             moment = null;
  174: 
  175:             if (!CanResolveCore())
  176:             {
  177:                 return false;
  178:             }
  179: 
  180:             if (!Pulse.TryComplete())
  181:             {
  182:                 return false;
  183:             }
  184: 
  185:             moment =
  186:                 new Moment(
  187:                     Pulse.Id,
  188:                     MomentOutcome.Completed,
  189:                     result,
  190:                     null,
  191:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  192: 
  193:             return true;
  194:         }
  195: 
  196:         private bool TryCreateFailedMoment(
  197:             IWorkUnit failedWork,
  198:             out IMoment? moment)
  199:         {
  200:             moment = null;
  201: 
  202:             var exception =
  203:                 failedWork.Exception;
  204: 
  205:             if (exception == null)
  206:             {
  207:                 return false;
  208:             }
  209: 
  210:             if (!Pulse.TryFail())
  211:             {
  212:                 return false;
  213:             }
  214: 
  215:             moment =
  216:                 new Moment(
  217:                     Pulse.Id,
  218:                     MomentOutcome.Failed,
  219:                     null,
  220:                     exception,
  221:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  222: 
  223:             return true;
  224:         }
  225: 
  226:         private bool TryCreateCancelledMoment(
  227:             out IMoment? moment)
  228:         {
  229:             moment = null;
  230: 
  231:             if (!Pulse.TryCancel())
  232:             {
  233:                 return false;
  234:             }
  235: 
  236:             moment =
  237:                 new Moment(
  238:                     Pulse.Id,
  239:                     MomentOutcome.Cancelled,
  240:                     null,
  241:                     null,
  242:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  243: 
  244:             return true;
  245:         }
  246: 
  247:         private bool CanResolveCore()
  248:         {
  249:             if (Pulse.Status !=
  250:                 PulseStatus.Processing)
  251:             {
  252:                 return false;
  253:             }
  254: 
  255:             if (_workUnits.Count == 0)
  256:             {
  257:                 return false;
  258:             }
  259: 
  260:             for (
  261:                 var index = 0;
  262:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  263:                 index++)
  264:             {
  265:                 if (_workUnits[index].Status !=
  266:                     WorkUnitStatus.Completed)
  267:                 {
  268:                     return false;
  269:                 }
  270:             }
  271: 
  272:             return true;
  273:         }
  274: 
  275:         private bool CanFinalizeCore()
  276:         {
  277:             if (Pulse.Status !=
  278:                 PulseStatus.Processing)
  279:             {
  280:                 return false;
  281:             }
  282: 
  283:             if (_workUnits.Count == 0)
  284:             {
  285:                 return false;
  286:             }
  287: 
  288:             for (
  289:                 var index = 0;
  290:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  291:                 index++)
  292:             {
  293:                 if (!_workUnits[index].IsTerminal)
  294:                 {
  295:                     return false;
  296:                 }
  297:             }
  298: 
  299:             return true;
  300:         }
  301: 
  302:         private IWorkUnit? GetFirstFailedWorkUnit()
  303:         {
  304:             for (
  305:                 var index = 0;
  306:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  307:                 index++)
  308:             {
  309:                 var workUnit =
  310:                     _workUnits[index];
  311: 
  312:                 if (workUnit.Status ==
  313:                     WorkUnitStatus.Failed)
  314:                 {
  315:                     return workUnit;
  316:                 }
  317:             }
  318: 
  319:             return null;
  320:         }
  321: 
  322:         private bool ContainsCancelledWorkUnit()
  323:         {
  324:             for (
  325:                 var index = 0;
  326:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  327:                 index++)
  328:             {
  329:                 if (_workUnits[index].Status ==
  330:                     WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled)
  331:                 {
  332:                     return true;
  333:                 }
  334:             }
  335: 
  336:             return false;
  337:         }
  338: 
  339:         private Guid[] GetWorkUnitIds()
  340:         {
  341:             return _workUnits
  342:                 .Select(
  343:                     workUnit =>
  344:                         workUnit.Id)
  345:                 .ToArray();
  346:         }
  347:     }
  348: }

CURRENT WILDS QSL ADAPTER

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Qsl\WildsQslExecutor.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using QSL.Core;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Qsl
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Executes QSL requests through the complete
   11:     /// WildsEngine request lifecycle.
   12:     ///
   13:     /// request
   14:     ///     -> Pulse
   15:     ///     -> WorkUnit
   16:     ///     -> QSL preparation
   17:     ///     -> QSL evaluation
   18:     ///     -> Moment
   19:     ///     -> Timeline
   20:     ///     -> Epoch
   21:     ///
   22:     /// Wilds owns the processing and temporal lifecycle.
   23:     /// QSL owns consumer binding, attached state,
   24:     /// language evaluation, diagnostics, and provenance.
   25:     /// </summary>
   26:     public sealed class WildsQslExecutor
   27:     {
   28:         private readonly QslEngine _qsl;
   29: 
   30:         private readonly Core.WildsEngine _wilds;
   31: 
   32:         public WildsQslExecutor(
   33:             QslEngine qsl,
   34:             Core.WildsEngine wilds)
   35:         {
   36:             _qsl =
   37:                 qsl
   38:                 ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(
   39:                     nameof(qsl));
   40: 
   41:             _wilds =
   42:                 wilds
   43:                 ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(
   44:                     nameof(wilds));
   45:         }
   46: 
   47:         /// <summary>
   48:         /// Executes ordinary QSL source through Wilds.
   49:         ///
   50:         /// This overload preserves the existing
   51:         /// source-only integration path.
   52:         /// </summary>
   53:         public ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
   54:             string source,
   55:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
   56:         {
   57:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   58:                     source))
   59:             {
   60:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   61:                     "QSL source cannot be empty.",
   62:                     nameof(source));
   63:             }
   64: 
   65:             return _wilds.ExecuteAsync(
   66:                 source,
   67:                 ExecuteSourceAsync,
   68:                 cancellationToken);
   69:         }
   70: 
   71:         /// <summary>
   72:         /// Executes a complete QSL request containing
   73:         /// source, consumer bindings, and state packages
   74:         /// through Wilds.
   75:         /// </summary>
   76:         public ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
   77:             WildsQslRequest request,
   78:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
   79:         {
   80:             if (request == null)
   81:             {
   82:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   83:                     nameof(request));
   84:             }
   85: 
   86:             return _wilds.ExecuteAsync(
   87:                 request,
   88:                 ExecuteRequestAsync,
   89:                 cancellationToken);
   90:         }
   91: 
   92:         private ValueTask<object?> ExecuteSourceAsync(
   93:             object? request,
   94:             CancellationToken cancellationToken)
   95:         {
   96:             cancellationToken
   97:                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
   98: 
   99:             var source =
  100:                 request as string;
  101: 
  102:             if (source == null)
  103:             {
  104:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  105:                     "WildsQslExecutor expected QSL source.");
  106:             }
  107: 
  108:             return EvaluateAsync(
  109:                 source);
  110:         }
  111: 
  112:         private ValueTask<object?> ExecuteRequestAsync(
  113:             object? request,
  114:             CancellationToken cancellationToken)
  115:         {
  116:             cancellationToken
  117:                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  118: 
  119:             var qslRequest =
  120:                 request as WildsQslRequest;
  121: 
  122:             if (qslRequest == null)
  123:             {
  124:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  125:                     "WildsQslExecutor expected a " +
  126:                     "WildsQslRequest.");
  127:             }
  128: 
  129:             for (
  130:                 var index = 0;
  131:                 index < qslRequest.Consumers.Count;
  132:                 index++)
  133:             {
  134:                 _qsl.BindConsumer(
  135:                     qslRequest.Consumers[index]);
  136:             }
  137: 
  138:             for (
  139:                 var index = 0;
  140:                 index < qslRequest.StatePackages.Count;
  141:                 index++)
  142:             {
  143:                 _qsl.ReceiveState(
  144:                     qslRequest.StatePackages[index]);
  145:             }
  146: 
  147:             cancellationToken
  148:                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  149: 
  150:             return EvaluateAsync(
  151:                 qslRequest.Source);
  152:         }
  153: 
  154:         private ValueTask<object?> EvaluateAsync(
  155:             string source)
  156:         {
  157:             var result =
  158:                 _qsl.Evaluate(
  159:                     source);
  160: 
  161:             if (!result.Succeeded)
  162:             {
  163:                 throw new WildsQslExecutionException(
  164:                     result);
  165:             }
  166: 
  167:             return new ValueTask<object?>(
  168:                 result.Value);
  169:         }
  170:     }
  171: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Qsl\WildsQslExecutionException.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using QSL.Core;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Qsl
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents an unsuccessful QSL processing result
    8:     /// encountered while resolving a Wilds WorkUnit.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Throwing this exception allows WildsEngine to record
   11:     /// the QSL failure as a failed WorkUnit, failed Pulse,
   12:     /// and failed Moment.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class WildsQslExecutionException :
   15:         Exception
   16:     {
   17:         public WildsQslExecutionException(
   18:             QslProcessingResult result)
   19:             : base(
   20:                 CreateMessage(
   21:                     result),
   22:                 result != null
   23:                     ? result.Exception
   24:                     : null)
   25:         {
   26:             Result = result ??
   27:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   28:                     nameof(result));
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public QslProcessingResult Result { get; }
   32: 
   33:         private static string CreateMessage(
   34:             QslProcessingResult result)
   35:         {
   36:             if (result == null)
   37:             {
   38:                 return "QSL processing failed without a result.";
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             if (result.HasException &&
   42:                 result.Exception != null)
   43:             {
   44:                 return
   45:                     "QSL processing raised an exception: " +
   46:                     result.Exception.Message;
   47:             }
   48: 
   49:             if (result.ParseFailed)
   50:             {
   51:                 return
   52:                     "QSL parsing failed with " +
   53:                     result.ParserDiagnostics.Count +
   54:                     " diagnostic(s).";
   55:             }
   56: 
   57:             if (result.EvaluationFailed)
   58:             {
   59:                 return
   60:                     "QSL evaluation failed with " +
   61:                     result.RuntimeDiagnostics.Count +
   62:                     " runtime diagnostic(s).";
   63:             }
   64: 
   65:             return "QSL processing did not succeed.";
   66:         }
   67:     }
   68: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Qsl\WildsQslRequest.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using QSL.Core.Runtime.Context;
    4: using QSL.Core.Runtime.Packages;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Qsl
    7: {
    8:     /// <summary>
    9:     /// Represents one complete QSL request submitted
   10:     /// through the Wilds processing lifecycle.
   11:     ///
   12:     /// Wilds treats this request as opaque runtime data.
   13:     /// The Wilds/QSL integration layer prepares QSL from
   14:     /// the supplied consumer bindings, state packages,
   15:     /// and source.
   16:     /// </summary>
   17:     public sealed class WildsQslRequest
   18:     {
   19:         private readonly QslConsumerBinding[] _consumers;
   20: 
   21:         private readonly QslStatePackage[] _statePackages;
   22: 
   23:         public WildsQslRequest(
   24:             string source,
   25:             IEnumerable<QslConsumerBinding>? consumers = null,
   26:             IEnumerable<QslStatePackage>? statePackages = null)
   27:         {
   28:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   29:                     source))
   30:             {
   31:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   32:                     "QSL source cannot be empty.",
   33:                     nameof(source));
   34:             }
   35: 
   36:             Source =
   37:                 source;
   38: 
   39:             _consumers =
   40:                 Copy(
   41:                     consumers,
   42:                     nameof(consumers));
   43: 
   44:             _statePackages =
   45:                 Copy(
   46:                     statePackages,
   47:                     nameof(statePackages));
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         public string Source { get; }
   51: 
   52:         public IReadOnlyList<QslConsumerBinding> Consumers =>
   53:             _consumers;
   54: 
   55:         public IReadOnlyList<QslStatePackage> StatePackages =>
   56:             _statePackages;
   57: 
   58:         private static T[] Copy<T>(
   59:             IEnumerable<T>? values,
   60:             string parameterName)
   61:             where T : class
   62:         {
   63:             if (values == null)
   64:             {
   65:                 return Array.Empty<T>();
   66:             }
   67: 
   68:             var items =
   69:                 new List<T>();
   70: 
   71:             foreach (var value in values)
   72:             {
   73:                 if (value == null)
   74:                 {
   75:                     throw new ArgumentException(
   76:                         "Request collections cannot contain null.",
   77:                         parameterName);
   78:                 }
   79: 
   80:                 items.Add(
   81:                     value);
   82:             }
   83: 
   84:             return items.ToArray();
   85:         }
   86:     }
   87: }

    1: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    2: using WildsEngine.Tests;
    3: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
