    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    7: using WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Fixtures;
    8: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    9: using WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
   10: using Xunit;
   11: 
   12: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
   13: {
   14:     /// <summary>
   15:     /// Certifies that WildsEngine.ExecuteAsync owns the
   16:     /// complete request lifecycle:
   17:     ///
   18:     /// request
   19:     ///     -> Pulse
   20:     ///     -> WorkUnit
   21:     ///     -> coordinator
   22:     ///     -> Moment
   23:     ///     -> Timeline
   24:     ///     -> Epoch
   25:     /// </summary>
   26:     public sealed class WildsEngineExecuteIntegrationTests
   27:     {
   28:         [Fact]
   29:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncRecordsSuccessfulMoment()
   30:         {
   31:             TestLog.Section(
   32:                 "WildsEngine ExecuteAsync Integration");
   33: 
   34:             TestLog.Test(
   35:                 "Successful request becomes completed Moment");
   36: 
   37:             var component =
   38:                 CreateComponent(
   39:                     "execute.success");
   40: 
   41:             using var engine =
   42:                 CreateEngine(
   43:                     component);
   44: 
   45:             Assert.True(
   46:                 engine.Initialize());
   47: 
   48:             var request =
   49:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   50:                     "request");
   51: 
   52:             var result =
   53:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   54:                     "result");
   55: 
   56:             var moment =
   57:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
   58:                         request,
   59:                         (
   60:                             observedRequest,
   61:                             cancellationToken) =>
   62:                         {
   63:                             cancellationToken
   64:                                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
   65: 
   66:                             Assert.Same(
   67:                                 request,
   68:                                 observedRequest);
   69: 
   70:                             return new ValueTask<object?>(
   71:                                 result);
   72:                         });
   73: 
   74:             Assert.Equal(
   75:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   76:                 moment.Outcome);
   77: 
   78:             Assert.Same(
   79:                 result,
   80:                 moment.Result);
   81: 
   82:             Assert.Null(
   83:                 moment.Exception);
   84: 
   85:             Assert.Single(
   86:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
   87: 
   88:             Assert.Equal(
   89:                 1,
   90:                 engine.Timeline.Count);
   91: 
   92:             Assert.Same(
   93:                 moment,
   94:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
   95: 
   96:             Assert.Equal(
   97:                 1,
   98:                 engine.Epoch.Count);
   99: 
  100:             Assert.Same(
  101:                 engine.Timeline,
  102:                 engine.Epoch.Timelines[0]);
  103: 
  104:             TestLog.State(
  105:                 $"Moment recorded | " +
  106:                 $"Outcome={moment.Outcome} | " +
  107:                 $"TimelineMoments={engine.Timeline.Count}");
  108: 
  109:             TestLog.Pass(
  110:                 "ExecuteAsync completed and recorded request history");
  111:         }
  112: 
  113:         [Fact]
  114:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncPreservesArbitraryRuntimeIdentity()
  115:         {
  116:             TestLog.Test(
  117:                 "ExecuteAsync preserves arbitrary runtime identity");
  118: 
  119:             var component =
  120:                 CreateComponent(
  121:                     "execute.identity");
  122: 
  123:             using var engine =
  124:                 CreateEngine(
  125:                     component);
  126: 
  127:             Assert.True(
  128:                 engine.Initialize());
  129: 
  130:             var runtimeObject =
  131:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
  132:                     "consumer-owned-object");
  133: 
  134:             var moment =
  135:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  136:                         runtimeObject,
  137:                         (
  138:                             request,
  139:                             cancellationToken) =>
  140:                         {
  141:                             cancellationToken
  142:                                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  143: 
  144:                             return new ValueTask<object?>(
  145:                                 request);
  146:                         });
  147: 
  148:             Assert.Equal(
  149:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
  150:                 moment.Outcome);
  151: 
  152:             Assert.Same(
  153:                 runtimeObject,
  154:                 moment.Result);
  155: 
  156:             Assert.Same(
  157:                 runtimeObject,
  158:                 engine.Timeline
  159:                     .Moments[0]
  160:                     .Result);
  161: 
  162:             TestLog.Pass(
  163:                 "Exact consumer runtime object survived engine execution");
  164:         }
  165: 
  166:         [Fact]
  167:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncRecordsFailedMoment()
  168:         {
  169:             TestLog.Test(
  170:                 "Execution failure becomes failed Moment");
  171: 
  172:             var component =
  173:                 CreateComponent(
  174:                     "execute.failure");
  175: 
  176:             using var engine =
  177:                 CreateEngine(
  178:                     component);
  179: 
  180:             Assert.True(
  181:                 engine.Initialize());
  182: 
  183:             var exception =
  184:                 new ExpectedExecutionException(
  185:                     "Expected integration failure.");
  186: 
  187:             var moment =
  188:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  189:                         "failure-request",
  190:                         (
  191:                             _,
  192:                             _) =>
  193:                         {
  194:                             throw exception;
  195:                         });
  196: 
  197:             Assert.Equal(
  198:                 MomentOutcome.Failed,
  199:                 moment.Outcome);
  200: 
  201:             Assert.Null(
  202:                 moment.Result);
  203: 
  204:             Assert.Same(
  205:                 exception,
  206:                 moment.Exception);
  207: 
  208:             Assert.Single(
  209:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  210: 
  211:             Assert.Single(
  212:                 engine.Timeline.Moments);
  213: 
  214:             Assert.Same(
  215:                 moment,
  216:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
  217: 
  218:             TestLog.Expected(
  219:                 exception.Message);
  220: 
  221:             TestLog.Pass(
  222:                 "Execution failure was retained as history");
  223:         }
  224: 
  225:         [Fact]
  226:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncRecordsCancelledMoment()
  227:         {
  228:             TestLog.Test(
  229:                 "Execution cancellation becomes cancelled Moment");
  230: 
  231:             var component =
  232:                 CreateComponent(
  233:                     "execute.cancellation");
  234: 
  235:             using var engine =
  236:                 CreateEngine(
  237:                     component);
  238: 
  239:             Assert.True(
  240:                 engine.Initialize());
  241: 
  242:             using var cancellation =
  243:                 new CancellationTokenSource();
  244: 
  245:             cancellation.Cancel();
  246: 
  247:             var operationWasCalled =
  248:                 false;
  249: 
  250:             var moment =
  251:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  252:                         "cancellation-request",
  253:                         (
  254:                             _,
  255:                             _) =>
  256:                         {
  257:                             operationWasCalled = true;
  258: 
  259:                             return new ValueTask<object?>(
  260:                                 "must-not-complete");
  261:                         },
  262:                         cancellation.Token);
  263: 
  264:             Assert.False(
  265:                 operationWasCalled);
  266: 
  267:             Assert.Equal(
  268:                 MomentOutcome.Cancelled,
  269:                 moment.Outcome);
  270: 
  271:             Assert.Null(
  272:                 moment.Result);
  273: 
  274:             Assert.Null(
  275:                 moment.Exception);
  276: 
  277:             Assert.Single(
  278:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  279: 
  280:             Assert.Single(
  281:                 engine.Timeline.Moments);
  282: 
  283:             Assert.Same(
  284:                 moment,
  285:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
  286: 
  287:             TestLog.Pass(
  288:                 "Cancellation was retained as history");
  289:         }

  291:         [Fact]
  292:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncRecordsMomentsInRequestOrder()
  293:         {
  294:             TestLog.Test(
  295:                 "Successive requests preserve Timeline order");
  296: 
  297:             var component =
  298:                 CreateComponent(
  299:                     "execute.timeline-order");
  300: 
  301:             using var engine =
  302:                 CreateEngine(
  303:                     component);
  304: 
  305:             Assert.True(
  306:                 engine.Initialize());
  307: 
  308:             var first =
  309:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  310:                         "first",
  311:                         (
  312:                             _,
  313:                             _) =>
  314:                             new ValueTask<object?>(
  315:                                 "first-result"));
  316: 
  317:             var second =
  318:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  319:                         "second",
  320:                         (
  321:                             _,
  322:                             _) =>
  323:                             new ValueTask<object?>(
  324:                                 "second-result"));
  325: 
  326:             Assert.Equal(
  327:                 2,
  328:                 engine.Timeline.Count);
  329: 
  330:             Assert.Same(
  331:                 first,
  332:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
  333: 
  334:             Assert.Same(
  335:                 second,
  336:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[1]);
  337: 
  338:             Assert.Equal(
  339:                 "first-result",
  340:                 first.Result);
  341: 
  342:             Assert.Equal(
  343:                 "second-result",
  344:                 second.Result);
  345: 
  346:             Assert.NotEqual(
  347:                 first.PulseId,
  348:                 second.PulseId);
  349: 
  350:             TestLog.State(
  351:                 "Timeline retained two Moments in request order");
  352: 
  353:             TestLog.Pass(
  354:                 "Request ordering was preserved");
  355:         }

  394:         [Fact]
  395: public async Task
  396:     TopologySelectedNeighborSurvivesCompleteWildsExecutionRoute()
  397: {
  398:     TestLog.Section(
  399:         "WildsEngine Topology / ExecuteAsync Integration");
  400: 
  401:     TestLog.Test(
  402:         "Topology-selected Neighbor survives the complete Wilds lifecycle");
  403: 
  404:     var topology =
  405:         new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  406: 
  407:     TestLog.Task(
  408:         "Building directional topology through A.State.Process().");
  409: 
  410:     Assert.True(
  411:         topology.A.State.Process(
  412:             new AddNeighborInstruction(
  413:                 topology.B,
  414:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  415:                 6.0)));
  416: 
  417:     Assert.True(
  418:         topology.A.State.Process(
  419:             new AddNeighborInstruction(
  420:                 topology.C,
  421:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  422:                 2.0)));
  423: 
  424:     Assert.True(
  425:         topology.A.State.Process(
  426:             new AddNeighborInstruction(
  427:                 topology.D,
  428:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership,
  429:                 1.0)));
  430: 
  431:     Assert.Equal(
  432:         3,
  433:         topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  434: 
  435:     var sourceBefore =
  436:         topology.A.Neighbors
  437:             .Relationships
  438:             .Select(
  439:                 relationship =>
  440:                     (
  441:                         relationship.Neighbor.Id,
  442:                         relationship.Membership,
  443:                         relationship.Distance))
  444:             .ToArray();
  445: 
  446:     TestLog.State(
  447:         $"Source topology contains {sourceBefore.Length} relationships");
  448: 
  449:     TestLog.Task(
  450:         "Selecting the nearest local Neighbor through the existing operator pipeline.");
  451: 
  452:     var pipeline =
  453:         new NeighborPipeline(
  454:             new MembershipNeighborFilter(
  455:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership),
  456:             new NearestNeighborOrder());
  457: 
  458:     var selectedRelationships =
  459:         topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  460:             pipeline);
  461: 
  462:     Assert.Equal(
  463:         2,
  464:         selectedRelationships.Count);
  465: 
  466:     Assert.Equal(
  467:         topology.C.Id,
  468:         selectedRelationships[0].Neighbor.Id);
  469: 
  470:     Assert.Equal(
  471:         topology.B.Id,
  472:         selectedRelationships[1].Neighbor.Id);
  473: 
  474:     var selectedNeighbor =
  475:         selectedRelationships[0].Neighbor;
  476: 
  477:     Assert.Same(
  478:         topology.C,
  479:         selectedNeighbor);
  480: 
  481:     TestLog.State(
  482:         $"Topology selected {selectedNeighbor.Slug}");
  483: 
  484:     var component =
  485:         CreateComponent(
  486:             "execute.topology-selection");
  487: 
  488:     using var engine =
  489:         CreateEngine(
  490:             component);
  491: 
  492:     Assert.True(
  493:         engine.Initialize());
  494: 
  495:     TestLog.Task(
  496:         "Submitting the exact topology-selected Neighbor to Wilds.");
  497: 
  498:     var moment =
  499:         await engine.ExecuteAsync(
  500:             selectedNeighbor,
  501:             (
  502:                 observedNeighbor,
  503:                 cancellationToken) =>
  504:             {
  505:                 cancellationToken
  506:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  507: 
  508:                 TestLog.State(
  509:                     "Selected Neighbor entered Wilds execution");
  510: 
  511:                 Assert.Same(
  512:                     selectedNeighbor,
  513:                     observedNeighbor);
  514: 
  515:                 Assert.Same(
  516:                     topology.C,
  517:                     observedNeighbor);
  518: 
  519:                 return new ValueTask<object?>(
  520:                     observedNeighbor);
  521:             });
  522: 
  523:     TestLog.Task(
  524:         "Verifying the selected Neighbor survived Wilds temporal resolution.");
  525: 
  526:     Assert.Equal(
  527:         MomentOutcome.Completed,
  528:         moment.Outcome);
  529: 
  530:     Assert.Null(
  531:         moment.Exception);
  532: 
  533:     Assert.Single(
  534:         moment.WorkUnitIds);
  535: 
  536:     Assert.Same(
  537:         selectedNeighbor,
  538:         moment.Result);
  539: 
  540:     Assert.Same(
  541:         topology.C,
  542:         moment.Result);
  543: 
  544:     Assert.Equal(
  545:         1,
  546:         engine.Timeline.Count);
  547: 
  548:     Assert.Same(
  549:         moment,
  550:         engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
  551: 
  552:     Assert.Same(
  553:         selectedNeighbor,
  554:         engine.Timeline
  555:             .Moments[0]
  556:             .Result);
  557: 
  558:     Assert.Equal(
  559:         1,
  560:         engine.Epoch.Count);
  561: 
  562:     Assert.Same(
  563:         engine.Timeline,
  564:         engine.Epoch.Timelines[0]);
  565: 
  566:     Assert.Single(
  567:         engine.Epoch
  568:             .Timelines[0]
  569:             .Moments);
  570: 
  571:     Assert.Same(
  572:         moment,
  573:         engine.Epoch
  574:             .Timelines[0]
  575:             .Moments[0]);
  576: 
  577:     Assert.Same(
  578:         selectedNeighbor,
  579:         engine.Epoch
  580:             .Timelines[0]
  581:             .Moments[0]
  582:             .Result);
  583: 
  584:     TestLog.Pass(
  585:         "Exact topology-selected Neighbor survived " +
  586:         "Wilds execution and temporal history.");
  587: 
  588:     TestLog.Task(
  589:         "Verifying execution did not mutate the source topology.");
  590: 
  591:     var sourceAfter =
  592:         topology.A.Neighbors
  593:             .Relationships
  594:             .Select(
  595:                 relationship =>
  596:                     (
  597:                         relationship.Neighbor.Id,
  598:                         relationship.Membership,
  599:                         relationship.Distance))
  600:             .ToArray();
  601: 
  602:     Assert.Equal(
  603:         sourceBefore,
  604:         sourceAfter);
  605: 
  606:     Assert.Equal(
  607:         3,
  608:         topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  609: 
  610:     Assert.True(
  611:         topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  612:             topology.B.Id,
  613:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  614: 
  615:     Assert.True(
  616:         topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  617:             topology.C.Id,
  618:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  619: 
  620:     Assert.True(
  621:         topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  622:             topology.D.Id,
  623:             NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership));
  624: 
  625:     TestLog.Pass(
  626:         "Wilds execution preserved the source Neighbor topology.");
  627: 
  628:     TestLog.Pass(
  629:         "Topology-to-execution identity certification completed successfully.");
  630: }
  631: 
  632: [Fact]
  633: public async Task
  634:     ConcurrentExecuteAsyncRecordsEveryMomentExactlyOnce()
  635: {
  636:     const int requestCount =
  637:         64;
  638: 
  639:     TestLog.Section(
  640:         "WildsEngine ExecuteAsync Concurrency");
  641: 
  642:     TestLog.Test(
  643:         "Concurrent requests record every Moment exactly once");
  644: 
  645:     var component =
  646:         CreateComponent(
  647:             "execute.concurrent-history");
  648: 
  649:     using var engine =
  650:         CreateEngine(
  651:             component);
  652: 
  653:     Assert.True(
  654:         engine.Initialize());
  655: 
  656:     var requests =
  657:         Enumerable
  658:             .Range(
  659:                 0,
  660:                 requestCount)
  661:             .Select(
  662:                 index =>
  663:                     new TestRuntimeObject(
  664:                         $"concurrent-request-{index:D3}"))
  665:             .ToArray();
  666: 
  667:     var release =
  668:         new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(
  669:             TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
  670: 
  671:     var enteredCount =
  672:         0;
  673: 
  674:     TestLog.Task(
  675:         $"Submitting {requestCount} concurrent requests.");
  676: 
  677:     var executions =
  678:         requests
  679:             .Select(
  680:                 request =>
  681:                     engine.ExecuteAsync(
  682:                             request,
  683:                             async (
  684:                                 observedRequest,
  685:                                 cancellationToken) =>
  686:                             {
  687:                                 cancellationToken
  688:                                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  689: 
  690:                                 Assert.Same(
  691:                                     request,
  692:                                     observedRequest);
  693: 
  694:                                 Interlocked.Increment(
  695:                                     ref enteredCount);
  696: 
  697:                                 await release.Task
  698:                                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  699: 
  700:                                 cancellationToken
  701:                                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  702: 
  703:                                 return observedRequest;
  704:                             })
  705:                         .AsTask())
  706:             .ToArray();
  707: 
  708:     TestLog.State(
  709:         $"Submitted requests = {executions.Length}");
  710: 
  711:     Assert.Equal(
  712:         requestCount,
  713:         executions.Length);
  714: 
  715:     release.TrySetResult(
  716:         true);
  717: 
  718:     var moments =
  719:         await Task.WhenAll(
  720:                 executions)
  721:             .ConfigureAwait(false);
  722: 
  723:     TestLog.State(
  724:         $"Entered operations = {enteredCount}");
  725: 
  726:     TestLog.State(
  727:         $"Returned Moments = {moments.Length}");
  728: 
  729:     Assert.Equal(
  730:         requestCount,
  731:         enteredCount);
  732: 
  733:     Assert.Equal(
  734:         requestCount,
  735:         moments.Length);
  736: 
  737:     TestLog.Task(
  738:         "Verifying every concurrent request completed successfully.");
  739: 
  740:     Assert.All(
  741:         moments,
  742:         moment =>
  743:         {
  744:             Assert.Equal(
  745:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
  746:                 moment.Outcome);
  747: 
  748:             Assert.Null(
  749:                 moment.Exception);
  750: 
  751:             Assert.NotNull(
  752:                 moment.Result);
  753: 
  754:             Assert.Single(
  755:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  756:         });
  757: 
  758:     TestLog.Pass(
  759:         "Every concurrent request produced a completed Moment.");
  760: 
  761:     TestLog.Task(
  762:         "Verifying request and result identity remained aligned.");
  763: 
  764:     for (var index = 0;
  765:          index < requestCount;
  766:          index++)
  767:     {
  768:         Assert.Same(
  769:             requests[index],
  770:             moments[index].Result);
  771:     }
  772: 
  773:     TestLog.Pass(
  774:         "Every Moment retained its exact request object as the result.");
  775: 
  776:     TestLog.Task(
  777:         "Verifying Pulse identities are unique.");
  778: 
  779:     var pulseIds =
  780:         moments
  781:             .Select(
  782:                 moment =>
  783:                     moment.PulseId)
  784:             .ToArray();
  785: 
  786:     Assert.Equal(
  787:         requestCount,
  788:         pulseIds.Distinct().Count());
  789: 
  790:     Assert.DoesNotContain(
  791:         Guid.Empty,
  792:         pulseIds);
  793: 
  794:     TestLog.Pass(
  795:         "Every concurrent request received a unique Pulse identity.");
  796: 
  797:     TestLog.Task(
  798:         "Verifying WorkUnit identities are unique.");
  799: 
  800:     var workUnitIds =
  801:         moments
  802:             .SelectMany(
  803:                 moment =>
  804:                     moment.WorkUnitIds)
  805:             .ToArray();
  806: 
  807:     Assert.Equal(
  808:         requestCount,
  809:         workUnitIds.Length);
  810: 
  811:     Assert.Equal(
  812:         requestCount,
  813:         workUnitIds.Distinct().Count());
  814: 
  815:     Assert.DoesNotContain(
  816:         Guid.Empty,
  817:         workUnitIds);
  818: 
  819:     TestLog.Pass(
  820:         "Every concurrent request received one unique WorkUnit identity.");
  821: 
  822:     TestLog.Task(
  823:         "Verifying Timeline recorded every returned Moment exactly once.");
  824: 
  825:     Assert.Equal(
  826:         requestCount,
  827:         engine.Timeline.Count);
  828: 
  829:     var recordedMoments =
  830:         engine.Timeline
  831:             .Moments
  832:             .ToArray();
  833: 
  834:     Assert.Equal(
  835:         requestCount,
  836:         recordedMoments.Length);
  837: 
  838:     Assert.Equal(
  839:         requestCount,
  840:         recordedMoments
  841:             .Select(
  842:                 moment =>
  843:                     moment.Id)
  844:             .Distinct()
  845:             .Count());
  846: 
  847:     foreach (var returnedMoment in moments)
  848:     {
  849:         Assert.Equal(
  850:             1,
  851:             recordedMoments.Count(
  852:                 recordedMoment =>
  853:                     ReferenceEquals(
  854:                         returnedMoment,
  855:                         recordedMoment)));
  856:     }
  857: 
  858:     TestLog.Pass(
  859:         "Timeline recorded every returned Moment exactly once.");
  860: 
  861:     TestLog.Task(
  862:         "Verifying every submitted request appears exactly once in history.");
  863: 
  864:     foreach (var request in requests)
  865:     {
  866:         Assert.Equal(
  867:             1,
  868:             recordedMoments.Count(
  869:                 moment =>
  870:                     ReferenceEquals(
  871:                         request,
  872:                         moment.Result)));
  873:     }
  874: 
  875:     TestLog.Pass(
  876:         "Timeline retained every submitted request exactly once.");
  877: 
  878:     TestLog.Task(
  879:         "Verifying Epoch retained the complete shared Timeline.");
  880: 
  881:     Assert.Equal(
  882:         1,
  883:         engine.Epoch.Count);
  884: 
  885:     Assert.Single(
  886:         engine.Epoch.Timelines);
  887: 
  888:     Assert.Same(
  889:         engine.Timeline,
  890:         engine.Epoch.Timelines[0]);
  891: 
  892:     Assert.Equal(
  893:         requestCount,
  894:         engine.Epoch
  895:             .Timelines[0]
  896:             .Moments
  897:             .Count);
  898: 
  899:     foreach (var returnedMoment in moments)
  900:     {
  901:         Assert.Equal(
  902:             1,
  903:             engine.Epoch
  904:                 .Timelines[0]
  905:                 .Moments
  906:                 .Count(
  907:                     recordedMoment =>
  908:                         ReferenceEquals(
  909:                             returnedMoment,
  910:                             recordedMoment)));
  911:     }
  912: 
  913:     TestLog.Pass(
  914:         "Epoch retained the complete concurrent Timeline.");
  915: 
  916:     TestLog.State(
  917:         $"Requests = {requestCount}");
  918: 
  919:     TestLog.State(
  920:         $"Unique Pulses = {pulseIds.Distinct().Count()}");
  921: 
  922:     TestLog.State(
  923:         $"Unique WorkUnits = {workUnitIds.Distinct().Count()}");
  924: 
  925:     TestLog.State(
  926:         $"Timeline Moments = {engine.Timeline.Count}");
  927: 
  928:     TestLog.State(
  929:         $"Epoch Timelines = {engine.Epoch.Count}");
  930: 
  931:     TestLog.Pass(
  932:         "Concurrent ExecuteAsync temporal-history certification completed successfully.");
  933: }
  934:

  941:                 {

  943:                         "certification.execute",
  944: 
  945:                     RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
  946:                         1,
  947: 
  948:                     ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
  949:                         1,
  950: 
  951:                     RetainedTaskProcessors =
  952:                         1,
  953: 
  954:                     ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
  955:                         1
  956:                 });
  957:         }

  963:                 layer);

  145:     public static class WildsQslCertification
  146:     {
  147:         private const string SuiteFileName =
  148:             "qsl_core_statement_testing.json";
  149: 
  150:         private const string ReportFileName =
  151:             "wilds_qsl_core_statement_testing_output.json";
  152: 
  153:         private static readonly List<string> Passed =
  154:             new List<string>();
  155: 
  156:         public static async Task<WildsQslCertificationReport> RunAllAsync()
  157:         {
  158:             Passed.Clear();
  159: 
  160:             var suitePath =
  161:                 Path.Combine(
  162:                     AppContext.BaseDirectory,
  163:                     "Certification",
  164:                     SuiteFileName);
  165: 
  166:             var suite =
  167:                 LoadSuite(
  168:                     suitePath);
  169: 
  170:             var qslEngine =
  171:                 CreateQslEngine();
  172: 
  173:             var component =
  174:                 CreateComponent(
  175:                     "certification.qsl.core");
  176: 
  177:             using var wildsEngine =
  178:                 CreateWildsEngine(
  179:                     component);
  180: 
  181:             Assert(
  182:                 wildsEngine.Initialize(),
  183:                 "WildsEngine initialization should succeed.");
  184: 
  185:             var executor =
  186:                 new WildsQslExecutor(
  187:                     qslEngine,
  188:                     wildsEngine);
  189: 
  190:             var report =
  191:                 new WildsQslCertificationReport();
  192: 
  193:             TestLog.Write(
  194:                 "=== Wilds / QSL Core Certification ===");
  195: 
  196:             TestLog.State(
  197:                 "Route: QSL source -> WildsEngine -> " +
  198:                 "Pulse -> WorkUnit -> Wilds/QSL integration -> " +
  199:                 "QSL -> WorkUnit result -> Moment -> " +
  200:                 "Timeline -> Epoch");
  201: 
  202:             TestLog.State(
  203:                 "Suite: " +
  204:                 suitePath);
  205: 
  206:             TestLog.State(
  207:                 "Tests: " +
  208:                 suite.Tests.Count);
  209: 
  210:             Console.WriteLine();
  211: 
  212:             for (
  213:                 var index = 0;
  214:                 index < suite.Tests.Count;
  215:                 index++)
  216:             {
  217:                 var test =
  218:                     suite.Tests[index];
  219: 
  220:                 var result =
  221:                     await ExecuteTestAsync(
  222:                             executor,
  223:                             wildsEngine,
  224:                             test)
  225:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  226: 
  227:                 report.Results.Add(
  228:                     result);
  229: 
  230:                 switch (result.Status)
  231:                 {
  232:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed:
  233:                         report.Passed++;
  234:                         Passed.Add(
  235:                             CreateTestName(
  236:                                 index,
  237:                                 test));
  238:                         break;
  239: 
  240:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency:
  241:                         report.MissingDependencies++;
  242:                         break;
  243: 
  244:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed:
  245:                         report.Failed++;
  246:                         break;
  247: 
  248:                     default:
  249:                         throw new InvalidOperationException(
  250:                             "Unknown certification status: " +
  251:                             result.Status);
  252:                 }
  253: 
  254:                 if (result.MomentOutcome ==
  255:                     MomentOutcome.Completed.ToString())
  256:                 {
  257:                     report.CompletedMoments++;
  258:                 }
  259: 
  260:                 if (result.MomentOutcome ==
  261:                     MomentOutcome.Failed.ToString())
  262:                 {
  263:                     report.FailedMoments++;
  264:                 }
  265: 
  266:                 Console.Write(
  267:                     result.Status switch
  268:                     {
  269:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed => ".",
  270:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency => "M",
  271:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed => "F",
  272:                         _ => "?"
  273:                     });
  274: 
  275:                 if ((index + 1) % 50 == 0)
  276:                 {
  277:                     Console.WriteLine();
  278:                 }
  279:             }
  280: 
  281:             if (suite.Tests.Count % 50 != 0)
  282:             {
  283:                 Console.WriteLine();
  284:             }
  285: 
  286:             report.Total =
  287:                 report.Results.Count;
  288: 
  289:             report.TimelineMoments =
  290:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  291: 
  292:             report.EpochTimelines =
  293:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count;
  294: 
  295:             ValidateCompleteHistory(
  296:                 suite,
  297:                 wildsEngine);
  298: 
  299:             WriteSummary(
  300:                 report);
  301: 
  302:             if (report.Failed > 0)
  303:             {
  304:                 WriteFailures(
  305:                     report.Results);
  306:             }
  307: 
  308:             if (report.MissingDependencies > 0)
  309:             {
  310:                 WriteMissingDependencies(
  311:                     report.Results);
  312:             }
  313: 
  314:             WriteReport(
  315:                 report);
  316: 
  317:             Assert(
  318:                 report.Total ==
  319:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  320:                 "Certification report must contain one result " +
  321:                 "for every suite case.");
  322: 
  323:             Assert(
  324:                 report.Total ==
  325:                 report.Passed +
  326:                 report.Failed +
  327:                 report.MissingDependencies,
  328:                 "Every certification result must be accounted for " +
  329:                 "as passed, failed, or missing a dependency.");
  330: 
  331:             Assert(
  332:                 report.IsCertified,
  333:                 "Wilds / QSL core certification requires every " +
  334:                 "core case to pass with no missing dependencies.");
  335: 
  336:             TestLog.Write(
  337:                 "Wilds / QSL core certification completed: " +
  338:                 report.Passed +
  339:                 "/" +
  340:                 report.Total +
  341:                 " cases passed.");
  342: 
  343:             TestLog.State(
  344:                 "Certification status: " +
  345:                 GetCertificationStatus(
  346:                     report));
  347: 
  348:             TestLog.State(
  349:                 "Missing Dependencies: " +
  350:                 report.MissingDependencies);
  351: 
  352:             TestLog.State(
  353:                 "Completed Moments: " +
  354:                 report.CompletedMoments);
  355: 
  356:             TestLog.State(
  357:                 "Failed Moments: " +
  358:                 report.FailedMoments);
  359: 
  360:             TestLog.State(
  361:                 "Timeline Moments: " +
  362:                 report.TimelineMoments);
  363: 
  364:             TestLog.State(
  365:                 "Epoch Timelines: " +
  366:                 report.EpochTimelines);
  367: 
  368:             return report;
  369:         }
  370: 
  371:         private static WildsQslCertificationSuite LoadSuite(
  372:             string suitePath)
  373:         {
  374:             if (!File.Exists(
  375:                     suitePath))
  376:             {
  377:                 throw new FileNotFoundException(
  378:                     "Wilds / QSL certification suite " +
  379:                     "was not found.",
  380:                     suitePath);
  381:             }
  382: 
  383:             try
  384:             {
  385:                 var json =
  386:                     File.ReadAllText(
  387:                         suitePath);
  388: 
  389:                 var suite =
  390:                     JsonSerializer.Deserialize
  391:                         <WildsQslCertificationSuite>(
  392:                             json,
  393:                             new JsonSerializerOptions
  394:                             {
  395:                                 PropertyNameCaseInsensitive =
  396:                                     true
  397:                             });
  398: 
  399:                 if (suite == null)
  400:                 {
  401:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  402:                         "Certification suite deserialized to null.");
  403:                 }
  404: 
  405:                 if (suite.Tests.Count == 0)
  406:                 {
  407:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  408:                         "Certification suite contains no tests.");
  409:                 }
  410: 
  411:                 return suite;
  412:             }
  413:             catch (Exception exception)
  414:                 when (exception is not FileNotFoundException)
  415:             {
  416:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  417:                     "Certification suite could not be loaded.",
  418:                     exception);
  419:             }
  420:         }
  421: 
  422:         private static async Task<WildsQslCertificationResult>
  423:             ExecuteTestAsync(
  424:                 WildsQslExecutor executor,
  425:                 global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine wildsEngine,
  426:                 WildsQslCertificationTest test)
  427:         {
  428:             var expectsError =
  429:                 string.Equals(
  430:                     test.Category,
  431:                     "Error",
  432:                     StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
  433: 
  434:             var timelineBefore =
  435:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  436: 
  437:             IMoment moment;
  438: 
  439:             try
  440:             {
  441:                 /*
  442:                  * This is the important certification boundary.
  443:                  *
  444:                  * The adapter submits the QSL source through
  445:                  * WildsEngine.ExecuteAsync.
  446:                  *
  447:                  * Wilds owns the Pulse and WorkUnit lifecycle.
  448:                  * QSL performs delegated computation while the
  449:                  * WorkUnit is processing.
  450:                  *
  451:                  * The QSL result returns to Wilds before Wilds
  452:                  * completes the WorkUnit and resolves the terminal
  453:                  * Moment.
  454:                  */
  455:                 moment =
  456:                     await executor.ExecuteAsync(
  457:                             test.Statement)
  458:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  459:             }
  460:             catch (Exception exception)
  461:             {
  462:                 /*
  463:                  * WildsEngine.ExecuteAsync must convert delegated
  464:                  * processing failures into failed WorkUnits and
  465:                  * failed Moments.
  466:                  *
  467:                  * An exception escaping this route means Wilds
  468:                  * did not resolve the request into honest temporal
  469:                  * history.
  470:                  */
  471:                 return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  472:                 {
  473:                     Category =
  474:                         test.Category,
  475: 
  476:                     Statement =
  477:                         test.Statement,
  478: 
  479:                     Expected =
  480:                         test.Expected,
  481: 
  482:                     Output =
  483:                         string.Empty,
  484: 
  485:                     Dependency =
  486:                         test.Dependency,
  487: 
  488:                     Status =
  489:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed,
  490: 
  491:                     Passed =
  492:                         false,
  493: 
  494:                     QslSucceeded =
  495:                         false,
  496: 
  497:                     EnteredWilds =
  498:                         true,
  499: 
  500:                     MomentCompleted =
  501:                         false,
  502: 
  503:                     TimelineRecorded =
  504:                         false,
  505: 
  506:                     EpochRecorded =
  507:                         false,
  508: 
  509:                     MomentOutcome =
  510:                         string.Empty,
  511: 
  512:                     ParseFailed =
  513:                         false,
  514: 
  515:                     EvaluationFailed =
  516:                         false,
  517: 
  518:                     ParserDiagnostics =
  519:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  520: 
  521:                     RuntimeDiagnostics =
  522:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  523: 
  524:                     Exception =
  525:                         exception.GetType().Name +
  526:                         ": " +
  527:                         exception.Message
  528:                 };
  529:             }
  530: 
  531:             var timelineAfter =
  532:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  533: 
  534:             var timelineRecorded =
  535:                 timelineAfter ==
  536:                 timelineBefore + 1 &&
  537:                 timelineAfter > 0 &&
  538:                 ReferenceEquals(
  539:                     moment,
  540:                     wildsEngine.Timeline
  541:                         .Moments[timelineAfter - 1]);
  542: 
  543:             var epochRecorded =
  544:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Count == 1 &&
  545:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count == 1 &&
  546:                 ReferenceEquals(
  547:                     wildsEngine.Timeline,
  548:                     wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines[0]) &&
  549:                 wildsEngine.Epoch
  550:                     .Timelines[0]
  551:                     .Moments
  552:                     .Count ==
  553:                 timelineAfter &&
  554:                 ReferenceEquals(
  555:                     moment,
  556:                     wildsEngine.Epoch
  557:                         .Timelines[0]
  558:                         .Moments[timelineAfter - 1]);
  559: 
  560:             if (moment.Outcome ==
  561:                 MomentOutcome.Completed)
  562:             {
  563:                 return CreateCompletedResult(
  564:                     test,
  565:                     moment,
  566:                     expectsError,
  567:                     timelineRecorded,
  568:                     epochRecorded);
  569:             }
  570: 
  571:             if (moment.Outcome ==
  572:                     MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  573:                 moment.Exception is
  574:                     WildsQslExecutionException qslException)
  575:             {
  576:                 return CreateFailedQslResult(
  577:                     test,
  578:                     moment,
  579:                     qslException,
  580:                     expectsError,
  581:                     timelineRecorded,
  582:                     epochRecorded);
  583:             }
  584: 
  585:             return CreateUnexpectedResult(
  586:                 test,
  587:                 moment,
  588:                 timelineRecorded,
  589:                 epochRecorded);
  590:         }
  591: 
  592:         private static WildsQslCertificationResult CreateCompletedResult(
  593:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
  594:             IMoment moment,
  595:             bool expectsError,
  596:             bool timelineRecorded,
  597:             bool epochRecorded)
  598:         {
  599:             var output =
  600:                 FormatValue(
  601:                     moment.Result);
  602: 
  603:             var passed =
  604:                 !expectsError &&
  605:                 moment.Exception == null &&
  606:                 moment.WorkUnitIds.Count == 1 &&
  607:                 timelineRecorded &&
  608:                 epochRecorded &&
  609:                 string.Equals(
  610:                     output,
  611:                     test.Expected,
  612:                     StringComparison.Ordinal);
  613: 
  614:             return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  615:             {
  616:                 Category =
  617:                     test.Category,
  618: 
  619:                 Statement =
  620:                     test.Statement,
  621: 
  622:                 Expected =
  623:                     test.Expected,
  624: 
  625:                 Output =
  626:                     output,
  627: 
  628:                 Dependency =
  629:                     test.Dependency,
  630: 
  631:                 Status =
  632:                     ClassifyResult(
  633:                         test,
  634:                         passed,
  635:                         dependencyMissing: false),
  636: 
  637:                 Passed =
  638:                     passed,
  639: 
  640:                 QslSucceeded =
  641:                     true,
  642: 
  643:                 EnteredWilds =
  644:                     true,
  645: 
  646:                 MomentCompleted =
  647:                     true,
  648: 
  649:                 TimelineRecorded =
  650:                     timelineRecorded,
  651: 
  652:                 EpochRecorded =
  653:                     epochRecorded,
  654: 
  655:                 MomentOutcome =
  656:                     moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  657: 
  658:                 ParseFailed =
  659:                     false,
  660: 
  661:                 EvaluationFailed =
  662:                     false,
  663: 
  664:                 ParserDiagnostics =
  665:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  666: 
  667:                 RuntimeDiagnostics =
  668:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  669: 
  670:                 Exception =
  671:                     null
  672:             };
  673:         }
  674: 
  675:         private static WildsQslCertificationResult CreateFailedQslResult(
  676:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
  677:             IMoment moment,
  678:             WildsQslExecutionException qslException,
  679:             bool expectsError,
  680:             bool timelineRecorded,
  681:             bool epochRecorded)
  682:         {
  683:             var processingResult =
  684:                 qslException.Result;
  685: 
  686:             var parserDiagnostics =
  687:                 CopyParserDiagnostics(
  688:                     processingResult);
  689: 
  690:             var runtimeDiagnostics =
  691:                 CopyRuntimeDiagnostics(
  692:                     processingResult);
  693: 
  694:             var dependencyMissing =
  695:                 IsDeclaredDependencyMissing(
  696:                     test,
  697:                     runtimeDiagnostics);
  698: 
  699:             var passed =
  700:                 expectsError &&
  701:                 !processingResult.Succeeded &&
  702:                 moment.Result == null &&
  703:                 moment.WorkUnitIds.Count == 1 &&
  704:                 timelineRecorded &&
  705:                 epochRecorded;
  706: 
  707:             return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  708:             {
  709:                 Category =
  710:                     test.Category,
  711: 
  712:                 Statement =
  713:                     test.Statement,
  714: 
  715:                 Expected =
  716:                     test.Expected,
  717: 
  718:                 Output =
  719:                     FormatValue(
  720:                         processingResult.Value),
  721: 
  722:                 Dependency =
  723:                     test.Dependency,
  724: 
  725:                 Status =
  726:                     ClassifyResult(
  727:                         test,
  728:                         passed,
  729:                         dependencyMissing),
  730: 
  731:                 Passed =
  732:                     passed &&
  733:                     !dependencyMissing,
  734: 
  735:                 QslSucceeded =
  736:                     processingResult.Succeeded,
  737: 
  738:                 EnteredWilds =
  739:                     true,
  740: 
  741:                 MomentCompleted =
  742:                     false,
  743: 
  744:                 TimelineRecorded =
  745:                     timelineRecorded,
  746: 
  747:                 EpochRecorded =
  748:                     epochRecorded,
  749: 
  750:                 MomentOutcome =
  751:                     moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  752: 
  753:                 ParseFailed =
  754:                     processingResult.ParseFailed,
  755: 
  756:                 EvaluationFailed =
  757:                     processingResult.EvaluationFailed,
  758: 
  759:                 ParserDiagnostics =
  760:                     parserDiagnostics,
  761: 
  762:                 RuntimeDiagnostics =
  763:                     runtimeDiagnostics,
  764: 
  765:                 Exception =
  766:                     processingResult.Exception?.Message ??
  767:                     qslException.Message
  768:             };
  769:         }
  770: 
  771:         private static WildsQslCertificationResult CreateUnexpectedResult(
  772:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
  773:             IMoment moment,
  774:             bool timelineRecorded,
  775:             bool epochRecorded)
  776:         {
  777:             return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  778:             {
  779:                 Category =
  780:                     test.Category,
  781: 
  782:                 Statement =
  783:                     test.Statement,
  784: 
  785:                 Expected =
  786:                     test.Expected,
  787: 
  788:                 Output =
  789:                     FormatValue(
  790:                         moment.Result),
  791: 
  792:                 Dependency =
  793:                     test.Dependency,
  794: 
  795:                 Status =
  796:                     WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed,
  797: 
  798:                 Passed =
  799:                     false,
  800: 
  801:                 QslSucceeded =
  802:                     false,
  803: 
  804:                 EnteredWilds =
  805:                     true,
  806: 
  807:                 MomentCompleted =
  808:                     false,
  809: 
  810:                 TimelineRecorded =
  811:                     timelineRecorded,
  812: 
  813:                 EpochRecorded =
  814:                     epochRecorded,
  815: 
  816:                 MomentOutcome =
  817:                     moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  818: 
  819:                 ParseFailed =
  820:                     false,
  821: 
  822:                 EvaluationFailed =
  823:                     false,
  824: 
  825:                 ParserDiagnostics =
  826:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  827: 
  828:                 RuntimeDiagnostics =
  829:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  830: 
  831:                 Exception =
  832:                     moment.Exception?.Message ??
  833:                     "Unexpected terminal Moment outcome: " +
  834:                     moment.Outcome
  835:             };
  836:         }
  837: 
  838:         private static void ValidateCompleteHistory(
  839:             WildsQslCertificationSuite suite,
  840:             global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine wildsEngine)
  841:         {
  842:             Assert(
  843:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count ==
  844:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  845:                 "Wilds Timeline must contain exactly one " +
  846:                 "Moment per certification statement.");
  847: 
  848:             Assert(
  849:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Count == 1,
  850:                 "The isolated certification Epoch must " +
  851:                 "contain exactly one Timeline.");
  852: 
  853:             Assert(
  854:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count == 1,
  855:                 "The certification Epoch Timeline collection " +
  856:                 "must contain exactly one Timeline.");
  857: 
  858:             Assert(
  859:                 ReferenceEquals(
  860:                     wildsEngine.Timeline,
  861:                     wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines[0]),
  862:                 "The Epoch must retain the engine Timeline.");
  863: 
  864:             Assert(
  865:                 wildsEngine.Epoch
  866:                     .Timelines[0]
  867:                     .Moments
  868:                     .Count ==
  869:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  870:                 "The Epoch must expose the complete " +
  871:                 "certification Moment history.");
  872:         }
  873: 
  874:         private static string[] CopyParserDiagnostics(
  875:             QslProcessingResult processingResult)
  876:         {
  877:             var diagnostics =
  878:                 new string[
  879:                     processingResult
  880:                         .ParserDiagnostics
  881:                         .Count
  882:                 ];
  883: 
  884:             for (
  885:                 var index = 0;
  886:                 index < diagnostics.Length;
  887:                 index++)
  888:             {
  889:                 diagnostics[index] =
  890:                     processingResult
  891:                         .ParserDiagnostics[index];
  892:             }
  893: 
  894:             return diagnostics;
  895:         }
  896: 
  897:         private static string[] CopyRuntimeDiagnostics(
  898:             QslProcessingResult processingResult)
  899:         {
  900:             var diagnostics =
  901:                 new string[
  902:                     processingResult
  903:                         .RuntimeDiagnostics
  904:                         .Count
  905:                 ];
  906: 
  907:             for (
  908:                 var index = 0;
  909:                 index < diagnostics.Length;
  910:                 index++)
  911:             {
  912:                 var entry =
  913:                     processingResult
  914:                         .RuntimeDiagnostics[index];
  915: 
  916:                 diagnostics[index] =
  917:                     "[" +
  918:                     entry.EntryType +
  919:                     "] " +
  920:                     entry.Source +
  921:                     ": " +
  922:                     entry.Message;
  923:             }
  924: 
  925:             return diagnostics;
  926:         }
  927: 
  928:         private static string FormatValue(
  929:             object? value)
  930:         {
  931:             if (value == null)
  932:             {
  933:                 return string.Empty;
  934:             }
  935: 
  936:             if (value is bool boolean)
  937:             {
  938:                 return boolean
  939:                     ? "true"
  940:                     : "false";
  941:             }
  942: 
  943:             if (value is string text)
  944:             {
  945:                 return text;
  946:             }
  947: 
  948:             if (value is char character)
  949:             {
  950:                 return character.ToString();
  951:             }
  952: 
  953:             if (value is IFormattable formattable)
  954:             {
  955:                 return formattable.ToString(
  956:                            null,
  957:                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ??
  958:                        string.Empty;
  959:             }
  960: 
  961:             return value.ToString() ??
  962:                    string.Empty;
  963:         }
  964: 
  965:         private static QslEngine CreateQslEngine()
  966:         {
  967:             var registry =
  968:                 new QslRuntimeRegistry();
  969: 
  970:             registry.RegisterDefaultEvaluators();
  971: 
  972:             var context =
  973:                 new QslRuntimeContext(
  974:                     registry);
  975: 
  976:             return new QslEngine(
  977:                 context);
  978:         }

  981:             string layer)
  982:         {
  983:             return new TestComponent(
  984:                 layer);
  985:         }

  987:         private static global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine
  988:             CreateWildsEngine(
  989:                 IComponent component)
  990:         {
  991:             return new global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine(
  992:                 component,
  993:                 new WildsEngineOptions
  994:                 {
  995:                     Layer =
  996:                         "certification.qsl.core",
  997: 
  998:                     RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
  999:                         1,
 1000: 
 1001:                     ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
 1002:                         1,
 1003: 
 1004:                     RetainedTaskProcessors =
 1005:                         1,
 1006: 
 1007:                     ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
 1008:                         1
 1009:                 });
 1010:         }

 1012:         private static string CreateTestName(
 1013:             int index,
 1014:             WildsQslCertificationTest test)

 1016:             return
 1017:                 (index + 1) +
 1018:                 ": [" +
 1019:                 test.Category +
 1020:                 "] " +
 1021:                 test.Statement;

 1024:         private static void WriteSummary(
 1025:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
 1026:         {
 1027:             Console.WriteLine();
 1028:             Console.WriteLine(
 1029:                 "========================================");
 1030:             Console.WriteLine(
 1031:                 "Total:                " +
 1032:                 report.Total);
 1033:             Console.WriteLine(
 1034:                 "Passed:               " +
 1035:                 report.Passed);
 1036:             Console.WriteLine(
 1037:                 "Failed:               " +
 1038:                 report.Failed);
 1039:             Console.WriteLine(
 1040:                 "Missing Dependencies: " +
 1041:                 report.MissingDependencies);
 1042:             Console.WriteLine(
 1043:                 "Completed Moments:    " +
 1044:                 report.CompletedMoments);
 1045:             Console.WriteLine(
 1046:                 "Failed Moments:       " +
 1047:                 report.FailedMoments);
 1048:             Console.WriteLine(
 1049:                 "Timeline Moments:     " +
 1050:                 report.TimelineMoments);
 1051:             Console.WriteLine(
 1052:                 "Epoch Timelines:      " +
 1053:                 report.EpochTimelines);
 1054:             Console.WriteLine(
 1055:                 "========================================");
 1056:         }
 1057: 
 1058:         private static void WriteFailures(
 1059:             IReadOnlyList<WildsQslCertificationResult> results)
 1060:         {
 1061:             Console.WriteLine();
 1062:             Console.WriteLine(
 1063:                 "Failures:");
 1064: 
 1065:             foreach (var result in results)
 1066:             {
 1067:                 if (result.Status !=
 1068:                     WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed)
 1069:                 {
 1070:                     continue;
 1071:                 }
 1072: 
 1073:                 WriteResultDetails(
 1074:                     result);
 1075:             }
 1076:         }
 1077: 
 1078:         private static void WriteMissingDependencies(
 1079:             IReadOnlyList<WildsQslCertificationResult> results)
 1080:         {
 1081:             Console.WriteLine();
 1082:             Console.WriteLine(
 1083:                 "Missing consumer dependencies:");
 1084: 
 1085:             foreach (var result in results)
 1086:             {
 1087:                 if (result.Status !=
 1088:                     WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency)
 1089:                 {
 1090:                     continue;
 1091:                 }
 1092: 
 1093:                 WriteResultDetails(
 1094:                     result);
 1095:             }
 1096:         }
 1097: 
 1098:         private static void WriteResultDetails(
 1099:             WildsQslCertificationResult result)
 1100:         {
 1101:             Console.WriteLine();
 1102:             Console.WriteLine(
 1103:                 "[" +
 1104:                 result.Category +
 1105:                 "] " +
 1106:                 result.Statement);
 1107: 
 1108:             Console.WriteLine(
 1109:                 "  Status: " +
 1110:                 result.Status);
 1111: 
 1112:             Console.WriteLine(
 1113:                 "  Dependency: " +
 1114:                 (result.Dependency ?? "<none>"));
 1115: 
 1116:             Console.WriteLine(
 1117:                 "  Expected: " +
 1118:                 (string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1119:                     result.Expected)
 1120:                     ? "<error>"
 1121:                     : result.Expected));
 1122: 
 1123:             Console.WriteLine(
 1124:                 "  Actual: " +
 1125:                 (string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1126:                     result.Output)
 1127:                     ? "<null>"
 1128:                     : result.Output));
 1129: 
 1130:             Console.WriteLine(
 1131:                 "  Outcome: " +
 1132:                 (string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1133:                     result.MomentOutcome)
 1134:                     ? "<none>"
 1135:                     : result.MomentOutcome));
 1136: 
 1137:             Console.WriteLine(
 1138:                 "  QSL succeeded: " +
 1139:                 result.QslSucceeded);
 1140: 
 1141:             Console.WriteLine(
 1142:                 "  Entered Wilds: " +
 1143:                 result.EnteredWilds);
 1144: 
 1145:             Console.WriteLine(
 1146:                 "  Moment completed: " +
 1147:                 result.MomentCompleted);
 1148: 
 1149:             Console.WriteLine(
 1150:                 "  Timeline recorded: " +
 1151:                 result.TimelineRecorded);
 1152: 
 1153:             Console.WriteLine(
 1154:                 "  Epoch recorded: " +
 1155:                 result.EpochRecorded);
 1156: 
 1157:             foreach (var diagnostic in
 1158:                      result.ParserDiagnostics)
 1159:             {
 1160:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1161:                     "  Parser: " +
 1162:                     diagnostic);
 1163:             }
 1164: 
 1165:             foreach (var diagnostic in
 1166:                      result.RuntimeDiagnostics)
 1167:             {
 1168:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1169:                     "  Runtime: " +
 1170:                     diagnostic);
 1171:             }
 1172: 
 1173:             if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1174:                     result.Exception))
 1175:             {
 1176:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1177:                     "  Exception: " +
 1178:                     result.Exception);
 1179:             }
 1180:         }
 1181: 
 1182:         private static void WriteReport(
 1183:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
 1184:         {
 1185:             var reportPath =
 1186:                 Path.Combine(
 1187:                     AppContext.BaseDirectory,
 1188:                     "Certification",
 1189:                     ReportFileName);
 1190: 
 1191:             try
 1192:             {
 1193:                 var reportDirectory =
 1194:                     Path.GetDirectoryName(
 1195:                         reportPath);
 1196: 
 1197:                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1198:                         reportDirectory))
 1199:                 {
 1200:                     Directory.CreateDirectory(
 1201:                         reportDirectory);
 1202:                 }
 1203: 
 1204:                 var reportJson =
 1205:                     JsonSerializer.Serialize(
 1206:                         report,
 1207:                         new JsonSerializerOptions
 1208:                         {
 1209:                             WriteIndented =
 1210:                                 true,
 1211: 
 1212:                             Converters =
 1213:                             {
 1214:                                 new JsonStringEnumConverter()
 1215:                             }
 1216:                         });
 1217: 
 1218:                 File.WriteAllText(
 1219:                     reportPath,
 1220:                     reportJson);
 1221: 
 1222:                 TestLog.State(
 1223:                     "Certification report: " +
 1224:                     reportPath);
 1225:             }
 1226:             catch (Exception exception)
 1227:             {
 1228:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
 1229:                     "Certification report could not be written.",
 1230:                     exception);
 1231:             }
 1232:         }
 1233: 
 1234:         private static WildsQslCertificationStatus ClassifyResult(
 1235:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
 1236:             bool behavioralPass,
 1237:             bool dependencyMissing)
 1238:         {
 1239:             if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
 1240:                     test.Dependency) &&
 1241:                 dependencyMissing)
 1242:             {
 1243:                 return WildsQslCertificationStatus
 1244:                     .MissingDependency;
 1245:             }
 1246: 
 1247:             return behavioralPass
 1248:                 ? WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed
 1249:                 : WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed;
 1250:         }
 1251: 
 1252:         private static bool IsDeclaredDependencyMissing(
 1253:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
 1254:             IReadOnlyList<string> runtimeDiagnostics)
 1255:         {
 1256:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
 1257:                     test.Dependency))
 1258:             {
 1259:                 return false;
 1260:             }
 1261: 
 1262:             var expected =
 1263:                 "Unresolved identifier: " +
 1264:                 test.Dependency;
 1265: 
 1266:             foreach (var diagnostic in runtimeDiagnostics)
 1267:             {
 1268:                 if (diagnostic.Contains(
 1269:                         expected,
 1270:                         StringComparison.Ordinal))
 1271:                 {
 1272:                     return true;
 1273:                 }
 1274:             }
 1275: 
 1276:             return false;
 1277:         }
 1278: 
 1279:         private static string GetCertificationStatus(
 1280:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
 1281:         {
 1282:             if (report.IsCertified)
 1283:             {
 1284:                 return "CERTIFIED";
 1285:             }
 1286: 
 1287:             if (report.Failed == 0 &&
 1288:                 report.MissingDependencies > 0)
 1289:             {
 1290:                 return
 1291:                     "INCOMPLETE - CONSUMER DEPENDENCIES MISSING";
 1292:             }
 1293: 
 1294:             return "NOT CERTIFIED";
 1295:         }
 1296: 
 1297:         private static void Assert(
 1298:             bool condition,
 1299:             string message)
 1300:         {
 1301:             if (!condition)
 1302:             {
 1303:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
 1304:                     message);
 1305:             }
 1306:         }
 1307:     }
 1308:     public static class WildsQslEndToEndCertification
 1309:     {
 1310:         public static async Task RunAllAsync()
 1311:         {
 1312:             TestLog.Write(
 1313:                 "=== Wilds / QSL End-to-End Certification ===");
 1314: 
 1315:             TestLog.State(
 1316:                 "Route: Wilds -> Pulse -> WorkUnit -> " +
 1317:                 "QSL -> WorkUnit result -> Moment -> " +
 1318:                 "Timeline -> Epoch");
 1319: 
 1320:             var tests =
 1321:                 new global::WildsEngine.Core.Tests
 1322:                     .WildsQslEndToEndTests();
 1323: 
 1324:             await tests
 1325:                 .QslWorkResolvesThroughCompleteWildsLifecycle()
 1326:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
 1327: 
 1328:             TestLog.Pass(
 1329:                 "Wilds / QSL end-to-end lifecycle certification");
 1330:         }
 1331:     }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\TestState.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.States;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
    6: 
    7: internal sealed class TestState :
    8:     WildsState
    9: {
   10:     public TestState(
   11:         IComponent component,
   12:         IContext context,
   13:         string layer)
   14:         : base(
   15:             component,
   16:             context,
   17:             layer)
   18:     {
   19:     }
   20: 
   21:     public override bool Process(
   22:         IStateInstruction instruction)
   23:     {
   24:         if (instruction == null)
   25:         {
   26:             throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                 nameof(instruction));
   28:         }
   29: 
   30:         if (TryProcessNeighborInstruction(
   31:                 instruction))
   32:         {
   33:             return true;
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         if (instruction is
   37:             ISetContextVariableInstruction
   38:             setContextVariable)
   39:         {
   40:             Context.SetVariable(
   41:                 setContextVariable.Key,
   42:                 setContextVariable.Value);
   43: 
   44:             return true;
   45:         }
   46: 
   47:         return false;
   48:     }
   49: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\TestLog.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Globalization;
    3: using System.IO;
    4: using System.Text;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Logging
    7: {
    8:     public static class TestLog
    9:     {
   10:         private static readonly object Sync = new object();
   11:         private static string? _path;
   12: 
   13:         public static string Path => _path ?? string.Empty;
   14: 
   15:         public static void Initialize(string? fileName = null)
   16:         {
   17:             lock (Sync)
   18:             {
   19:                 if (_path != null)
   20:                     return;
   21: 
   22:                 var logs = System.IO.Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Logs");
   23:                 Directory.CreateDirectory(logs);
   24:                 var name = fileName ?? $"wildsengine-tests-{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}.log";
   25:                 _path = System.IO.Path.Combine(logs, name);
   26:                 File.WriteAllText(_path, $"WildsEngine test log{Environment.NewLine}", Encoding.UTF8);
   27:             }
   28:         }
   29: 
   30:         public static void Section(string title)
   31:         {
   32:             Write("");
   33:             Write("============================================");
   34:             Write($" {title}");
   35:             Write("============================================");
   36:         }
   37: 
   38:         public static void Write(string message)
   39:         {
   40:             lock (Sync)
   41:             {
   42:                 if (_path == null)
   43:                     Initialize();
   44: 
   45:                 Console.WriteLine(message);
   46:                 File.AppendAllText(_path!,
   47:                     $"{DateTime.Now.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} {message}{Environment.NewLine}",
   48:                     Encoding.UTF8);
   49:             }
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         public static void Test(string message) => Write($"[TEST] {message}");
   53:         public static void Pass(string message) => Write($"[PASS] {message}");
   54:         public static void Task(string message) => Write($"    [TASK] {message}");
   55:         public static void State(string message) => Write($"    [STATE] {message}");
   56:         public static void Expected(string message) => Write($"    [EXPECTED] {message}");
   57:         public static void Fail(string message, Exception ex) =>
   58:             Write($"[FAIL] {message}: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}");
   59:     }
   60: }
